## Document 019: 20260803_投資週報_科技股財報表現分化，聯準會態度轉鷹

Source PDF: all report pdf/20260803_投資週報_科技股財報表現分化，聯準會態度轉鷹.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-08-03
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 8 月 3 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 聯準會模糊溝通引發長端殖利率攀升：FOMC 以 9:3 決議利率不變，聲明稿措辭幾無變動，重
申通膨相對偏高，堅守 2%通膨目標。本次聯準會維持利率的考量是強勁的經濟數據已推升殖
利率，達到收緊金融條件的效果，為聯準會爭取到維持利率不變的空間。不過聯準會主席華許
拒絕將本次決議視為「暫停」 ，且表達高度尊重市場訊息態度，隱含若9 月會議前就業數據穩
健、通膨未能持續降溫，將順應市場進行升息。
◼ 對科技股評價已由單純 「營收成長」 轉向檢視 「AI 變現效率與自由現金流品質」: 財報優於預

期的包括微軟其 Azure 雲端業務營收年增逾三成、亞馬遜的 AWS 業務成長加速、蘋果在
iPhone 銷售營收年增超過兩成；不如預期的包括 Meta 因資本支出上調致自由現金流下跌、
Alphabet 亦受資本支出擴張壓迫現金流，顯示市場開始檢驗 AI 變現能力。
◼ 美伊緊張局勢升溫：伊朗以飛彈攻擊美軍在中東的基地，美國隨即恢復對伊朗的空襲。川普表
示美方將非常猛烈地回擊 ； 伊朗則繼續攻擊美軍在周邊國家的部署地點 。 考量全球石油庫存來
到一年低點，衝突可能使油價波動擴大。
市場展望與操作策略
◼ 短線聚焦美股財報，科技股中期向上趨勢料不致有變：近期股市修正主要來自折現率、評價與
籌碼調整，目前尚未出現 AI 需求下降的證據，市場關注點從資本支出轉向對獲利與現金流的
延續性，如微軟在財報公布後大漲15%。半導體類股近期修正後，評價壓力已部分釋放，但技
術面仍未形成明確底部。既有核心持股不宜因短線波動而全面減碼，但建議降低高評價、高擁
擠度及高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集中度 。 此外 ， 投資人可以定時定額或利用拉回時分批布局具

獲利潛力的台、日股市。
◼ 3~10 年美國公債/投資級債為債市首選：在未來聯準會減少前瞻指引、利率路徑更依賴數據
環境下，市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將提高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資
人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較大波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風
險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險。因此，建議債券布
局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸引力的息收，亦能降低短端
政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

## Document 041: 2026年7月价格数据点评：输入性通胀影响缓解下价格数据均边际回落

2026 年 08 月 11 日

《再论股债跷跷板：条件相关、疤痕
效 应 与 负 反 馈 判 别— 固 收 专 题 》

-2026.8.3  
输入性通胀影响缓解下价格数据均边际回落 
——2026 年 7 月价格数据点评

陈曦（分析师） 王帅中（联系人）  
chenxi2@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790521100002 
wangshuaizhong@kysec.cn 
证书编号：S0790125070016

事件：国家统计局公布 2026 年 7 月价格数据，7 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.5%（前值为
+1.0%，下同） ，环比下降 0.1%（-0.3%） ；核心CPI 环比上涨 0.3%（-0.1%） ，同
比上涨 0.9%（+1.0%）；PPI 环比下降 0.7%（-0.3%） ，同比上涨 3.5%（+4.1%），
PPI 同比增速回落。 
 7 月价格数据关注点 
7 月 CPI 同比较 6 月有所下降，整体不及市场预期。7 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.5%，
较前值下降 0.5pct，Wind 统计 18 家机构预测 CPI 同比上涨平均值为 0.8%、 中位
数为 0.8%，CPI 同比增长整体不及市场预期，汽油价格涨幅回落或为主要原因。 
6 月原油价格中枢较高位回落，对 CPI 的上拉影响已经有所减弱，7 月国内汽油
价格滞后调整，汽油价格同比上涨 1.0%，涨幅比 6 月回落 16.0pct，对 CPI 的上
拉影响比 6 月减少约 0.45pct，食品项中猪肉价仍为拖累项，但降幅有所收窄，
猪肉价格下降 13.3%，降幅比 6 月收窄 2.6pct，影响 CPI 同比下降约 0.25pct，7
月底中央政治局会议中指出稳定生猪等农畜产品生产和价格，促进农民稳定增
收，生猪价格或已完成筑底迎来阶段性反弹。需要注意的是，AI 链条涨价持续，
计算机、平板电脑和移动电话机价格分别上涨17.4%、17.2%和 8.5%，涨幅均有
扩大，合计影响 CPI 同比上涨约 0.14pct，AI 产业链的发展或将进一步支撑 CPI
上涨。CPI 环比下降 0.1%，较前值回升 0.2pct，2021-2025 年 7 月 CPI 环比均
值为 0.38%，本次 CPI 环比低于季节性，主要受国际市场价格波动影响，输入
性通胀有所缓解。随着美伊局势逐渐缓和，国际油价中枢下降，7 月国内汽油价
格下降 10.7%，降幅比 6 月扩大 5.8pct，影响 CPI 环比下降约 0.35pct。生猪价格
环比上看已边际改善，猪肉价格由 6 月下降 0.8%转为上涨 4.1%，影响 CPI 环比
上涨约 0.07pct。 
7 月 PPI 环比下降 0.7%，同比上涨 3.5%，较前值下降 0.6pct，PPI 同比边际上
有所放缓，但依旧维持较高幅度涨幅，3 月 PPI 同比在连续下降 41 个月后首次
上涨，5 月 PPI 同比增长逼近 4%，6 月 PPI 同比增长站上 4%，7 月涨幅有所回
落，随着美伊局势缓和下国际油价中枢下移，同时有色金属价格基数抬升，PPI
同比或已见顶。Wind 统计 19 家机构预测 PPI 同比上涨平均值为 4.0%、中位数
为 3.8%，PPI 同比不及预期。PPI 环比下降 0.7%，较前值下降 0.4pct，6 月 PPI
环比再度落入负区间，7 月降幅有所扩大，2021-2025 年 7 月 CPI 环比均值为
-0.28%，本次 CPI 环比低于季节性。主要原因是美伊局势缓解，国际原油价格
中枢下移， 同时有色金属价格有所下降， 输入性通胀缓解下国内相关行业价格下
行， 石油相关的石油开采、 精炼石油产品制造、 有机化学原料制造价格分别下降
11.8%、8.4%和 4.2%， 有色金属相关的有色金属矿采选业、 有色金属冶炼和压延
加工业价格分别下降 2.1%和 1.7%， 这五个行业影响PPI 环比下降约 0.65pct，需
要注意的是， 尽管PPI 重新落入负区间， 但产业升级加快带动新兴行业需求增加，
价格上行，经济运行向新向优未发生改变。 
 物价回升有望构成 2026 年债券收益率上行的基本面 
债券收益率，由两个因素决定：一是物价（或物价预期）的方向，二是资金利率
的方向（央行的态度） 。其他因素例如财政、信用等则通过“经济（或预期）—
—物价（或预期） ” ，或者“资金利率的预期” ，两条路径传导至债市。我们认为，
当前的“潜在通胀”2%，或将成为 10 年国债收益率的 下限。  
 债市观点：在债市“资产荒”充分演绎后，应警惕债市调整风险 
2026 年上半年“资产荒”持续演绎，主要有三大因素驱动，资金利率持续下行、
非银久期处于低位、基本面数据走弱支撑债市走强。 
目前三大利多已经出现边际变化，警惕债市调整风险。 
资金面层面，4 月后资金利率持续回升，6 月以来 DR001 中枢稳定在 1.4%左右，
回归 2025 年下半年水平，市场宽松预期证伪；拥挤度层面，当前公募等非银机
构债券久期已达近年高位，存在抱团瓦解、估值回调风险；基本面层面，6 月起
政策边际加码、 油价冲击消退， 工业数据回暖，消费同比数据也或将随高基数效
应消退逐步修复，基本面由债市利多转为利空。此外，市场主流的“资产荒支撑
债牛”逻辑并不成立：贷款疲软是长期的新常态，且本轮债市上涨，由非银抱团
买入驱动，并非银行缺资产导致的配置行为。 
整体来看，在债市“资产荒”已经充分演绎之后，特别是如果降息预期发酵/或者
落地，应警惕债市调整风险。 
 风险提示：政策变化超预期；经济变化超预期。

评级 说明
证券评级
买入（Buy） 预计相对强于市场表现 20%以上；
增持（outperform） 预计相对强于市场表现 5%～20%；
中性（Neutral） 预计相对市场表现在－5%～＋5%之间波动；
减持（underperform） 预计相对弱于市场表现 5%以下。
行业评级
看好（overweight） 预计行业超越整体市场表现；
中性（Neutral） 预计行业与整体市场表现基本持平；
看淡（underperform） 预计行业弱于整体市场表现。
备注：评级标准为以报告日后的 6~12 个月内，证券相对于市场基准指数的涨跌幅表现，其中A 股基准指数为沪
深 300 指数、港股基准指数为恒生指数、新三板基准指数为三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的） 、 美股基准指数为标普500 或纳斯达克综合指数。我们在此提醒您， 不同证券研究机构采用不同
的评级术语及评级标准。我们采用的是相对评级体系，表示投资的相对比重建议；投资者买入或者卖出证券的决

定取决于个人的实际情况，比如当前的持仓结构以及其他需要考虑的因素。投资者应阅读整篇报告，以获取比较
完整的观点与信息，不应仅仅依靠投资评级来推断结论。
分析、估值方法的局限性说明
本报告所包含的分析基于各种假设， 不同假设可能导致分析结果出现重大不同。 本报告采用的各种估值方法及模型
均有其局限性，估值结果不保证所涉及证券能够在该价格交易。

台光電為國內專業銅箔基板廠商 ， 主要產品
包含銅箔基板 、 黏合片(PP)以及多層壓合基

(5-10%)，過去公司主要強項在無鹵素材料
以及手機 PP 配方，由於 PP 毛利率高於平
均且隨著 iPhone 滲透率提升帶動公司營

廖貫捷 kuanchieh.liao@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

台光電 (2383 TT) 
高速 CCL 王者，2026-2028 年獲利高速成長

永豐觀點 
台光電通吃 ASIC AI Server 訂單，高階料號市占率 38.6%全球第一，2Q26 毛利
率創高令人驚艷，中線在 800G、1.6T Switch 產品週期推升下 2026 年獲利持續
創高，長線 M9 料號貢獻，2026-2028 年獲利高速成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
台光電在 AI Server 接單無虞下產能吃緊，2Q26 毛利率創高來到 33.9%優於預
期，，長線而言M9 於 2H26 貢獻營運規格升級趨勢不變，公司亦規劃 2028 年底
月產能擴充至 1110 萬張較 2026 年增加 80.4%，營運價量齊揚態勢不變，研究部
上調 2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 115.37、211.35 以及 339.22 元，投資建議維持
買進，目標價上調至 6340 元(30X2027 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
台光電企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

台光電為國內專業銅箔基板廠商，主要產品包含銅箔基板、黏合片(PP)以及多層壓
合基板，2Q26 終端應用為行動裝置(10-15%)、基礎設施(75-80%)、以及汽車/工
業/其他(5-10%)，過去公司主要強項在無鹵素材料以及手機 PP 配方，由於 PP 毛
利率高於平均且隨著 iPhone 滲透率提升帶動公司營運逐年成長 ， 公司亦積極轉型
往基地台、Server 等網通以及車用相關應用發展，並成為通吃 GPU 及 ASIC AI 
server CCL 供應商。公司下游客戶為華通、臻鼎-KY、欣興以及健鼎等終端客戶包
含 Samsung、APPLE、華為、Cisco，主要競爭對手為聯茂、台燿、Doosan 以及
Panasonic。 
2Q26 毛利率 33.9%創高，本業表現令人驚豔：台光電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 27.55 元，
優於研究部原先預估的 22.15 元水準，其中毛利率較 1Q26 增加 4.5 個百分點來
到 33.9%創高並優於預期 3.1 個百分點，主要原因來自稼動率維持滿載、產品組合
轉佳以及漲價效應，其中受惠 AI server 需求暢旺，基礎設施占比來到 75-85%，
帶動 M7 以上高階料號佔比從 1Q26 的 55-60%進一步提升至 60-65%， 即便6 月
第二次漲價未完全反映，本業表現仍令人驚艷。 
AI 需求撐腰， 3Q26 營收 QoQ+21.1% ： 台光電 7 月營收 192.07 億元
(+8.3%MoM，+129.4%YoY)整體 3Q26 營收達成率 33.6%符合預期，研究部認
為 3Q26 除了 AI server 需求推動 M7 以上高階料號占比提升至 65-70%外，6 月
份 漲 價 效 應 亦 可 望 完 整 季 度 貢 獻 ， 預 估 營 收572.44 億元 (+21.1%QoQ ，
+127.7%YoY)，毛利率在稼動率滿載及產品組合轉佳下提升至 35.5%，稅後淨利
128.83 億元(+30.54%QoQ，+254.3%YoY)，單季稅後 EPS 35.96 元(原估 25.37
元)。 
2H26 仍具漲價空間，上調 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 115.37 元：台光電基礎設施產品
線毛利率優於公司平均水準，其中 AI server 占 55-60%、General server 占 15-
20%、Switch 占 15-20%、LEO 占 15-20%，整體櫃間傳輸以及 Agentic AI 帶動
的 General server 等泛 AI 需求達 75-80%，展望 2026 年不論 GPU 及 ASIC 陣營
相關需求不墜，稼動率持續維持滿載，另一方面 M7 以上 CCL 在供給吃緊下擴產
能見度已達 2028 年，2H26 仍具漲價空間，研究處上調台光電 2026 年營收至
1942.72 億元 (+106.1%YoY) ，毛利率 34.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利413.36 億元
(+182.2%YoY)，稅後 EPS 115.37 元(原估 84.89 元)。

M9 於 2H26 問世 ，CCL 料號升級趨勢不變： 台光電於2020 年來積極切入 Server、
Switch 以及 AI Server 等高速傳輸領域持續侵蝕同業聯茂(6213 TT) 和台燿(6274 
TT)於 Server 及 Switch 市占，2023 年並成為美系 AI Server 首輪供應商，帶動相
關基礎設施產線於2Q26 達 75-80%，毛利率超越 Handheld 成為全公司最佳，長
線而言隨著 ASIC AI Server 以及 800G、1.6T Switch 產品週期推升，M8 比重將於
2025 年攀升，而M9 亦將於 2H26 問世並占比低個位數，公司產品組合轉佳趨勢不
變。 
AI server 需求強勁，擴產規畫達 2028 年：隨著 AI Server 於高速傳輸需求殷切，
不論櫃內連結的 Switch Tray，或者機櫃間連接的乙太網路規格皆持續升級，另外
Agentic AI 亦帶動一般 server 快速成長，以 AMD 預期 2030 相關 CPU TAM 達
2000 以美元以上計算 CAGR 達 40-45%，且 Oak Stream 新平台下料號升級至
M7，整體高階料號供給吃緊，以目前土建 1.-1.5 年，設備交期 0.5-1.0 年計算，
新產能最快開出時點落於 2028 年，預計 4Q26 大園擴增 30 萬張新產能總計月產
能達 615 萬張(+17.1%YoY)，2027 年在中山、昆山以及檳城產能擴充下年底月產
能達 930 萬張(+51.2%YoY)，2028 年年底更上看 1110 萬張(+19.4%YoY)，考量
規格升級以及高階料號潛在供給吃緊後台光電 2027-2028 年獲利成長具上調空
間 ， 預估稅後EPS 分別上調至 211.35 元(原估 159.73 元)及 339.22 元(原估 247.13
元)。

Key observations on HK investors' positioning and interests
The ROIC of AI capex is the most important thing to track for
future hardware demand
For Greater China Semis, our major underlying assumptions for AI semis come from cloud
capex updates (Exhibit 5) and major CSPs' narratives on RoI (Exhibit 2). Some HK
investors told us that they are tracking the ARR of LLM vendors, on a quarterly or even
monthly basis, to make sure the revenue expansion is fast enough to cover the growing

capex costs. We alluded to Brian Nowak’s recent research: Internet: The Paths to 25-50%
GenAI ROIC (27 Jul 2026). And from the recent rise in cloud capex budgets, AI revenue
growth, as well as the positive narratives on CSPs' ROIC, we are not worried about
overproduction of AI GPUs and ASICs in 2027 –  our assessment of TSMC CoWoS capacity
implies a 70%-80% Y/Y increase in chip output in 2027.
While spending continues, how big the "memory cake"
should be is a growing debate

Some investors even believe that if memory’s portion of cloud capex budgets shifts to
computing and networking chips, AI capex may generate more computing capacity.  In our
cloud capex 2027 breakdown analysis (Exhibit 5), memory accounts for 53% of the
budget of US$1.5tn, while computing (GPUs, ASICs, CPUs) accounts for 20%.
Exhibit 1: 2027 Cloud Capex Breakdown by Using US$1.5 Trillion as the Total  Budget
(including sovereign AI)

The implied chip volume vs. electricity consumption (GW)
was frequently asked about, suggesting that power supply
remains a global bottleneck
When we showed our 2027 CoWoS implied chip volume (around 19 mn units), investors
were concerned about whether there would be sufficient electricity.  If each GPU/ASIC's

average TDP is 2kw, it would mean that around 38GW of new electricity would be
needed. Looking at it the other way around, when SpaceX indicated that it would expand
its capacity from 2GW by this year-end, to closer to 10GW as an ambitious target  (see
SpaceX: Strong Beat/Outlook vs. Higher Capex), we were also asked what that means to
TSMC’s CoWoS capacity consumption. Of note, Adam Jonas is only modeling in 3 GW of
incremental computing – getting to 5 GW by year-end 2027. But just in a rough sense, the

additional 8GW would mean around 4mn units of Rubin GPUs or 20% of the AI
accelerators that TSMC produces in 2027. See Exhibit 5 and Exhibit 2 for detailed
analysis.

Memory currently still attracts more attention than logic/

computing semis
HK investors were still more interests in the memory stocks than the logic foundries, such
as TSMC, ASE, and MediaTek. We believe it is because of more positioning with more
volatility in memory stocks. Some investors even stated a belief that “if memory stocks
don’t work, logic foundries like TSMC also can’t work.” Historically, the logic semi and
memory cycles  were indeed concurrent, if they shared the same end demand. However,

we can sense that this time around, there are memory-specific debates. For example, by
sharing the same AI infrastructure, memory is facing more “spec change.” For example, we
were cross-checked about the Rubin Ultra’s HBM spec change and the new Apple iPhone's
DRAM density being reduced from 12GB to 9GB. It seems that investors are trying to

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
figure out whether those spec changes have been caused by shortage or customers’ intent
to save costs.
Within memory, we see a split in participation between
momentum and value investors
In our Asian Tech strategist Shawn Kim’s report Asia Technology: Memory – A Small
Wrinkle (6 Aug 2026), he focused on the memory trade vs. valuation support. “The
sharpest correction in the memory industry to date appears to have ended, shifting focus

to buybacks as the next catalyst. ” We do think that momentum investors in HK are more
conservative on the memory stock price rebound – they are focusing on secondary
derivatives (eg. DRAM price Y/Y change), earnings estimate revision breadth, and spot
market trend. For example, when we gave our view about potential price moderation in
Chinese consumer NAND modules into 4Q26, some investors were concerned that it
may affect contract prices in 2027.

However, another camp of memory investors really appreciates the potential cash returns
from memory companies. They are willing to use P/E multiples to evaluate the stocks and
buy on recent dips.
What are key stocks to own, if  investors' focus shifts from
pricing hike to volume play?
We are still bullish on commodity semis such as DRAM/NAND and MLCC. However, some
momentum investors expect  consolidation. Instead, they turned their focus back to

volume plays or  business models, technology that can also add value or increase the
efficiency of AI infrastructure.
Key OWs in logic/compute
• We think TSMC’s wafer business and ASE’s packaging (along with ABF substrates)
are the key concepts of volume plays. Otherwise, we continue to like the ASIC
story (saving on budgets if successfully executed) and keep MediaTek as our Top
Pick.
• We also pushed the agenetic AI enablers CPU proxies in Greater China semis,

including Aspeed (server BMC), Hygon (Chinese CPU), GUC (Google Arm-based
CPU), and ASE (AMD server CPU).
Our calls in niche memories
• Within niche memory, we prefer DDR4 DRAM (OW Nanya Tech and Winbond) to
NAND module makers (EW Phison and Longsys).
Stocks to avoid
• We also suggest avoiding low-end commodity players that may not be able to hike
prices successfully – for example, GWC (EW rated) in raw wafers and Silergy (UW
rated) in consumer analog.

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
Key analysis: TSMC CoWoS vs. GW consumption
How significant is SpaceX's 8GW data center build in 2027?
During its  earnings call on August 4, SpaceX announced an exclusive commitment to build
its future computing capacity on Nvidia's Vera Rubin architecture. The scale is
unprecedented: 2 gigawatts (GW) of installed computing by 2026, scaling to around 10
GW by 2027.
If realized, this would represent a substantial expansion in SpaceX’s computing footprint

and could support increasingly computing-intensive AI workloads across its businesses.
The buildout would also create another sizable demand vector for NVIDIA accelerators
and the broader AI infrastructure supply chain, including memory, logic wafer supply,
networking, power, etc.
If we assume that all of the 8GW of computing will be Vera Rubin in 2027, it would
suggest around 400k of CoWoS wafer demand, accounting for close to half of Nvidia
Rubin's CoWoS booking in 2027. According to our latest CoWoS forecast, we are expecting

around 38GW of computing power to be produced from TSMC, while SpaceX's 8GW plan
could account for around 20% of total GPU/ASIC production.

Global CSPs kept raising capex during C2Q26 earnings
CSP capex continues to move higher as hyperscalers lean into AI infrastructure buildout
amid sustained demand and ongoing capacity constraints. Recent guidance revisions from
Amazon, Alphabet, and Meta point to a higher-for-longer investment cycle, while
Microsoft’s underlying infrastructure spending remains elevated despite accounting-
related changes in reported capex.
The key investor debate is increasingly shifting from whether AI demand is real to whether

incremental returns can keep pace with the magnitude of capital deployment. For now,
management commentary suggests that confidence remains high, with capacity largely
being absorbed as it comes online, but the rising spending profile raises the bar for future
cloud growth, AI monetization, and free cash flow conversion.
AI investments  are paying off, according to  CSPs' official
comments

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
During those CSPs' earnings calls, commentary continued to support the view that rising
AI capex is increasingly underwritten by visible monetization rather than purely
speculative demand. Below are key comments from those CSPs.
Meta: Mark Zuckerberg said “these AI investments are paying off” in the core ads business.
More importantly for the forward ROI debate, he argued that there should be “a
significantly higher margin on selling intelligence rather than selling compute directly. But

think that there's a big opportunity obviously to sell compute as well" and "quite
optimistic to see meaningful growth in all of these areas."
Google: On the capex/ROI question, CFO Anat Ashkenazi said the objective is to “invest as
long as we see an attractive return on that investment.” CEO Pichai added that Alphabet
has become more bullish on the AI opportunity over the past year, citing stronger
consumer, enterprise and developer momentum.
Amazon: Amazon disclosed that AWS's AI revenue run-rate is now above US$25bn and

growing at triple-digit rates YoY, after rising significantly QoQ. Amazon also said that AI
and traditional/core AWS workloads are increasingly reinforcing each other's growth.
Microsoft:- CFO Amy Hood said, “Customer demand continues to exceed available
capacity” and that incremental Azure capacity was “quickly monetized.”
Exhibit 6: Consensus CY27 capex just increased by 70%+ (or ~$500bn) YTD, which makes us believe that as long as the qualitative

commentary on cloud spending stays robust, there likely will be further increases in CY27 capex
Source: Company data, FactSet, Morgan Stanley Research. Note: Using 5/13/2025 CRVW estimates for 'beginning of 2025' and 'a year ago' as CRWV went public in March 2025.

Exhibit 7: Following US hyperscalers' C2Q26 earnings, our cloud capex tracker is now
pointing to 29% Y/Y in 2027, up 15 points from the prior forecast of +14% Y/Y about a
month ago
Source: Company data, FactSet, Morgan Stanley US Research. Note: Cloud capex includes capex from Alphabet, Amazon, Microsoft, Meta
Platforms, Alibaba, Tencent, Baidu, CoreWeave, Nebius, IREN, Tesla, Apple, IBM, and Oracle. We started to include NVIS from 2023, and CRWV/

IREN from 2024.
What if memory could  consume less of future cloud capex?
Assuming 2027 global cloud capex from major CSPs reaches US$1.5trn, we expect the mix
to remain heavily skewed toward memory and accelerated‑computing infrastructure,
underscoring the increasingly hardware‑intensive nature of AI investment. Memory
represents the largest component at >50% of total spending in our view, reflecting the
growing importance of HBM and other high‑bandwidth memory technologies with high

ASP driven by severe supply shortage.
GPU/ASIC spending excluding HBM accounts for 15–20%, while infrastructure contributes
20%, highlighting substantial investment in data center buildout, power, cooling, and
networking. Although current server CPU exposure remains relatively low, we believe it
will also enjoy rapid growth in the following years given strong agentic AI demand.
However, the current capex mix is heavily influenced by elevated memory pricing. If

memory prices normalize from current levels, we believe CSPs could reallocate a greater
share of budgets toward GPUs/ASICs and server CPUs, allowing the same overall capex
envelope to support more computing capacity. In this scenario, lower memory costs
could effectively improve the purchasing power of cloud capex and potentially accelerate
deployment of incremental AI computing.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 168: 3491昇達科 第一金

Source PDF: all report pdf/3491昇達科 第一金.pdf

### Page 1

周建霖 
(886-2)2563-1666 
mark.chou@firstsec.com.tw

昇達科(3491) 
買進, TP 1,880 (潛在報酬 39.26%)  
買進 
Price(08/06):1,350 
Target Price：1880

短期昇達科本身營運受 S 客戶新舊世代交替而導致出貨放緩，預
計 8 月起恢復動能，然而第 13 次 Starship 測試任務順利完成，
研究部維持正向看待下半年投入商轉後將加速 V3 衛星的部屬進
度。而A 客戶近期也加緊發射衛星 ，7 月底更又再向 FCC 申請 5105
顆 DTC 衛星的部屬計畫，可見整體低軌衛星市場在未來幾年仍處
於蓬勃發展期。綜合以上，研究部預估昇達科 2026 年 EPS 18.55
元，2027 年 EPS 37.55 元，目前 PE 為 36x，雖然短期衛星客戶
拉貨力道較為趨緩 ， 然而從產業面上的動態可知客戶們持續積極規
劃未來發展 ， 因此研究部仍看好昇達科長期營運表現將隨客戶步調
增長，因此維持 50xPER，目標價 1880 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
昇達科(3491)成立於 1999 年 ， 資本額6.75 億元 。 主要產品為高頻
通訊元件 ， 應用包含5G/4G 回傳網路 、 射頻天線及低軌衛星 ，2025
年佔營收比重各為 15.9%、21.6%、59%， 以及3.5%的網路工程。
公司生產基地包含台灣、越南。

⚫ 近期報酬率(%) ⚫ 營運概況 
受客戶新舊世代轉換，低軌營收成長動能暫時放緩 
昇達科 26Q2 營收 9.0 億元 ，Q-11.6%，Y+75.6%， 毛利率55.60%，
稅後淨利 3.1 億元，Q+21.6%，Y+333.2%，EPS 4.47 元。由於
客戶將來到新舊世代交替，下單力道暫時放緩，在手訂單較年初的
18 億降至 12 億，26Q2 低軌營收占比降至 74%，產品組合相對轉
差的情況下本業獲利明顯受到影響 ， 不過客戶對未出貨品的補貼挹
注下，26H1 整體稅後獲利仍優於 2025 全年表現。

圖二：昇達科低軌衛星營收(百萬)

資料來源：昇達科、第一金投顧彙整

斥資 17 億，提高巨頻持股改認合併報表 
7/15 昇達科發布重訊以每股 215 元 ， 不超過7554 張 ， 合計不超過
17 億元取得巨頻科技持股，將從原先持有 9.66%股權提高到
55.08%，由 FOCVI 改認為合併報表，巨頻科技主要從事高頻高速
互連產品之專業開發製造，產品涵蓋  RF/微波/毫米波連接方案、
高速訊號模組等，廣泛應用於資料中心高速傳輸、航太 /衛星、行
動通訊、半導體等領域，2025 年年營收約 6 億元，交易預計 8 月
完成，為了因應客戶需求，巨頻已投入6 億資本在高雄擴建新廠，
預計 28H1 完工投產，屆時產能將能提升目前的 2.5-3 倍，2026

### Page 3

本資料內容僅供參考，投資人應審慎考量自身之投資需求與風險，本公司恕不負任何法律責任，亦不作任何保證 
第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.) 台北市長安東路一段 35 號 3F   TEL(02) 2563 -1666 
營業執照字號:114 金管投顧新字第 013 號 
3 
年巨頻預計仍有成長 15-20%的機會，但進入 2027 年由於產能已
相對緊張，因此預計成長幅度相對受限。

8 月出貨動能恢復，預計 26H2 低軌營收 H+20% 
昇達科 7 月營收 2.3 億元，M-10.4%，Y+82.0%，客戶拉貨動能的
節奏在 7 月持續受到影響 ， 不過預計8 月起將逐步恢復 ， 目前在手
訂單金額約 7-8 億元，但預計 10 月將會有對於明年更長期的相關
合約簽訂，因此公司仍正向看待下半年低軌營收表現，預期將仍較
上半年成長 20%，產品組合改善後獲利表現也將同步回升。綜合
以上，研究部預估昇達科 2026 年營收 44.4 億元，Y+81.2%，毛
利率 58.83%，稅後淨利 12.8 億元，Y+146.3%，EPS 18.55 元。

A 客戶持續擴大衛星部屬規模，S 客戶 Starship 測試順利 
為了能在 2026 年開始在部分地區提出服務，A 客戶今年來已完成
216 顆衛星的部屬，至 7 月底合計 396 顆衛星運行中，此外更再
向 FCC 提出 5105 顆 DTC 服務的星座計畫，預計於 2028 年開始
部屬任務 ， 加計先前通過的二代星系A 客戶合計規劃共約 1.3 萬顆
衛星的規模。近期 S 客戶第 13 次測試任務於 7/23 順利完成，研
究部認為此有助於加速 Starship 投入正式任務中 ，對於 V3 衛星的
部屬進程將帶來實質性的貢獻。除了傳統衛星業務外，昇達科也開
始佈局毫米波功率放大器、太赫茲傳輸等相關技術，預計於 2028
年陸續開始有所貢獻。綜上所述，研究部預估昇達科 2027 年營收
82.1 億元，Y+84.8%，毛利率 62.77%，稅後淨利 25.8 億元，
Y+102.4%，EPS 37.55 元。

結論 
短期昇達科本身營運受 S 客戶新舊世代交替而導致出貨放緩，預
計 8 月起恢復動能，然而第 13 次 Starship 測試任務順利完成，研
究部維持正向看待下半年投入商轉後將加速 V3 衛星的部屬進度。

 절대 (달러환산) (11.3) (18.0) 83.1

2Q26 매출 49조, 영업이익 2.85조 기록 
원화 약세 수혜와 HEV 판매 호조로 인한 외형 성장을 기록했으나 영업이익 관점에서 1) 기
말환율 상승으로 인한 환이익 상쇄, 2) 고수익성 차량의 부품 공급 문제로 인한 감익, 3) 시
장 경쟁 격화로 인한 ASP 상승 대비 대당 인센티브 증가 (-0.4조). 
하반기 전사 OPM 개선 가능: 3분기 생산 정상화, 4분기 신차 효과 
3Q26F 부품 수급 이슈 해결하며 2Q26 대비 차량부문 OPM 개선 가능하며, 재고 
Restocking 수요 활용하여 인센티브 완화 가능할 것. 4Q26F 국내 주력모델인 투싼, 아반
떼 신형 모델과 제네시스 브랜드의 GV80 HEV, GV90의 본격 매출인식 반영을 통해 자동차
부문 OPM 본격적 개선이 가능할 것. 3Q26F의 생산 정상화로 인한 qoq 증익과 임금협상 
관련 일회성 비용으로 인한 감익의 비교가 중요할 것이며, 4Q26F 자동차부문 OPM 상승은 
신차효과 사이클이 본격화되는 FY27F 전사 수익성에 대한 힌트를 제공할 것. 
로보틱스 밸류에이션 부담 정상화 = 우선주 매력 부각 
본주의 우선주 대비 가격 프리미엄은 로보틱스 기대감이 최고치에 도달한 6월중 170%까지 
상승했으나 현재 110%까지 하락한 상황. 신사업 기반 밸류에이션 할증에 대한 기대감 (본
주 프리미엄의 상방을 제공하는 요소)과 손익비를 모두 고려할 시점. 하반기 hoh 증익과 
FY27F 수익성 회복은 연간 최소 16,000원 이상 주주환원 (2우B 종가 기준 7.7% 이상의 
연간 주주환원수익률)을 기록할 우선주의 투자매력도를 부각. 
투자의견 BUY로 상향, 목표주가는 570,000원으로 하향 
이전 보고서의 투자의견 하향 이후 주가의 낙폭 과대로 인한 상승여력이 확보된 상황. 로보
틱스로 인한 주가 급등이 높은 Target P/E를 정당화할 수 없다는 의견을 유지하며, 글로벌 
완성차 시장에 잔존하는 불확실성을 고려하여 SOTP 밸류에이션의 자동차부문 Target P/E 
또한 15x에서 10x로 하향.

## Document 231: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회한화에어로스페이스(012450) 자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회한화에어로스페이스(012450) 자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 8. 3  
자회사 실적 영향으로 컨센서스 상회

2Q26 Review: 상장 자회사 실적 영향 컨센서스 상회 
한화에어로스페이스 2분기 매출 9.3조원으로 전년비 47.3% 증가 영업이익도 1.4조
원(영업이익률 14.7%)으로 전년비 57.9% 증가. 컨센서스 대비 매출 23%, 영업이익 
37% 상회하는 수준. 이는 중복상장 자회사(한화오션/한화시스템) 실적 영향이 큰 것
으로 판단. 지상방산 부문 실적은 당사 예상치에 부합하는 수준. 국내 매출은 천호/천
검 등 양산 증가로 전년비 26.9% 증가, 해외 매출은 폴란드/이집트/중동 등 계획된 
납품 일정이 순조롭게 진행되면서 전년비 13.8% 증가. 사측은 하반기부터 폴란드 K9 
2차 양산 진행될 것이라고 밝힘 
하반기 방산 수주 증가 예상 
2분기 지상방산 부문 수주잔고는 38.3조원으로 전년비 20.7% 증가. 직전 4분기 누
적 지상방산 부문 매출 기준 4.5년치에 해당하는 수준. 2분기 중 핀란드 K9(9.400억
원)과 에스토니아 천무(약 1,000억원) 반영. 하반기 중 스페인 K9(약 6,000억원) 예
상되며 미국 K9 우선협상자 선정 및 폴란드 K9 3차 기대. 뿐만 아니라 사우디 MNG 
사업이나 중동/유럽 L-SAM 수주도 기대해 볼 수 있음 
자회사 주가 하락 및 순차입금 증가 영향으로 목표주가 하향 
자회사 주가 하락 및 순차입금 증가 영향으로 목표주가 1,650,000원으로 하향 조정
하나 향후 다양한 무기체계 수주 증가 기대되는 투자포인트는 유효하기 때문에 투자의
견 매수 유지. GTF 엔진 RSP 손실 감소 및 판매량 증가로 항공우주 부문 실적이 개선
되고 있는 점도 긍정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang @lssec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

## Document 271: A股牛市第三阶段的新叙事

Source PDF: all report pdf/A股牛市第三阶段的新叙事.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-08-15
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

国信证券表⽰，新叙事⼀是科技从上游到下游，从出⼝逻辑到国产替代，关注算⼒国产替
代、 AI 应⽤，以及创新药券商等科技延伸领域；⼆是内需的政策刺激和股价低位共振，出
⼝数据未来边际减弱，稳增⻓要内需接⼒，如⽩酒、地产等。
摘要：
核⼼结论：① 24 年 924 开启的⽜市进⼊第三阶段，市场调整后的新⼀轮上涨往往出现新叙事。②
新叙事 1 ：科技从上游到下游，从出⼝逻辑到国产替代，关注算⼒国产替代、 AI 应⽤，以及创新
药券商等科技延伸领域。③新叙事 2 ：内需的政策刺激和股价低位共振，出⼝数据未来边际减
弱，稳增⻓要内需接⼒，如⽩酒、地产等。
1.本轮⽜市步⼊第三阶段
A 股⽜市三阶段驱动⼒和⾏业主线有差异。⽜市有⾃⾝的周期规律，《荀⽟根讲策略：少即是多》
第 5 章通过对 A 股历史分析，提出⽜市三段论概念，各阶段驱动⼒和主线⾏业均有差异，可以归
纳为⼀阶段 “ 投低 ” 、⼆阶段 “ 投强 ” 、三阶段 “ 投顺 ” 。以 2019-21 年⽜市为例，三个阶段划分⻅下
图。
⽜市第⼀阶段是孕育期，对应波浪理论中的 1 浪上涨和 2 浪回调，政策宽松驱动估值修复；结构

上，市场最看重的是位置⽽⾮基本⾯，当⻛险偏好⾃低位回升时，低估资产普遍受益。第⼆阶
段是爆发期，即 3 浪上涨和 4 浪回调，盈利改善带来戴维斯双击；这时投资主线回归基本⾯，景
⽓度最⾼、业绩最强的板块成为⾏业主线。第三阶段即 5 浪冲顶，资⾦⼊场推⾼市场情绪，逻辑
顺畅、有想象空间的领域更受资⾦⻘睐。
本轮⽜市步⼊第三阶段，产业层⾯新叙事有望展开。就本轮⽜市⽽⾔， 24 年 924 政策转向推动⽜
市开启， 24/09-25/04 属于⽜市第⼀阶段，早期低估的⾦融地产、消费医药轮番修复，随后科技
成⻓接⼒⾛强。 25/4 开始 A 股净利润同⽐转正，盈利改善逐渐发⼒，⽜市步⼊第⼆阶段，基本⾯
优势最明显的科技涨幅居前，其中 25/04-26/07 期间通信最⼤涨幅 318% 、电⼦最⼤涨幅 235% ，
同期沪深 300 最⼤涨幅 41% 。
今年 7 ⽉调整以来，部分投资者的⽜市信念有所动摇。我们在《⽜未完，新⻛景 -20260718 》中
指出， 7 ⽉ A 股下跌属于⽜市中后期的正常调整 , 类⽐ 99 年 519 ⾏情， 26/1-26/7 的震荡调整类似

20/8-21/2 。当前政策环境仍宽松，宏微观基本⾯渐次修复，居⺠资⾦⼊市趋势加速， A 股⽜市正
逐渐步⼊第三阶段。⽜市第三阶段⾏业结构需重视 “ 投顺 ” ，结合当前政策背景和产业逻辑来看，
下半年科技扩散和内需演绎两⼤新叙事值得重视。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

2. 新叙事 1 ：科技从上游到下游，从出⼝逻辑到国产替代
AI 周期有望从算⼒硬件向应⽤演绎。回看 12-15 年移动互联浪潮，前期在 3G 商⽤、 iPhone 4S 发
布的背景下硬件端景⽓度更⾼， 13-14 年电⼦信息制造业规上利润增速和电⼦ ROE 持续增⻓，后
期 4G 全⾯商⽤、智能⼿机降价、 PC 软件实现在移动端的落地应⽤，共同推动移动互联产业周期
向应⽤端演绎， 15-16 年计算机、传媒景⽓度明显回暖。
站在当下 AI 浪潮中， 22 年 ChatGPT 问世以来， AI 算⼒需求和对⼤模型训练的存储需求同步激
增，推动上游算⼒硬件景⽓度和价格明显上升。上游成本的抬升、收⼊端增速慢于⽀出端等因
素影响下，云⼚商现⾦流情况逐步恶化，市场开始担忧⾼额资本开⽀的持续性， AI 硬件的叙事正
在松动。类似 12-15 年，若未来⼤模型成本下降、全新 AI 应⽤场景加速落地， AI 周期有望从硬件
端向应⽤端演绎。此外，随着中美科技领域贸易摩擦加剧，将推动 AI ⾏情从算⼒硬件向应⽤和国

产替代端扩散。

### Page 3

科技叙事或从出⼝扩散⾄国产替代和应⽤。借鉴 12-15 年经验，移动互联股市⾏情跟随产业周期
逐步从硬件扩散⾄应⽤。再看本轮 AI ⾏情， 22 年以来 AI 算⼒指数明显跑赢 AI 应⽤、恒⽣科技，
若基于 PEG 视⻆观察，机器⼈、应⽤软件等领域估值盈利性价⽐逐渐显现，未来 AI ⾏情有望出
现扩散，具体关注以下细分⽅向：
①算⼒国产替代： 8 ⽉美国联邦通信委员会（ FCC ）宣布拟采取禁⽌进⼝中国产新型光收发模块
等⼀系列制裁措施。后续在国内技术进步 + 政策⽀持⾃主可控催化下，贸易环境的不确定性或使
得科技叙事逐步从出⼝科技转向内需科技，关注存储、算⼒、半导体设备材料领域的国产替代
机会。
② AI 应⽤： AI 多模态⼤模型加速演进，有望在⾦融、传媒、消费等多个场景中持续渗透，同时近
期众多具⾝智能相关产品正逐渐从 B 端⾛向 C 端，叠加宇树科技 A 股上市在即，互联⽹平台、⼈
形机器⼈、智能驾驶等应⽤⽅向或迎来爆发增⻓期。
③创新药和券商：这两个领域属于科技的延伸。创新药属于成⻓投资⻛格，今年以来创新药出

海势头不减， BD 交易⾦额和丰富度持续提升，具有基本⾯优势的创新药有望获得市场再定价。
此外，⽜市中后期成交额逐渐放⼤，同时科技企业 IPO 回暖也拓宽券商营收，盈利改善趋势明显
的券商同样具备配置价值。

### Page 4

3. 新叙事 2 ：内需的政策刺激和股价低位共振
上半年国内经济 K 型分化，下半年内需政策有望加码。上半年国内三驾⻢⻋表现分化，其中出⼝
累计同⽐为 17.60% 、社零累计同⽐为 1.30% 、投资累计同⽐为 -5.70% ， GDP 增⻓主要靠出⼝拉
动，内需不⾜仍是当前经济运⾏的主要⽭盾。展望下半年，在去年同期的出⼝⾼基数叠加贸易
⻛险加剧的背景下，出⼝对经济的拉动效应可能边际递减。 26Q2 的实际 GDP 同⽐增速为 4.3% ，

### Page 5

为近四年最低⽔平，也低于全年 4.5%-5.0% 的⽬标区间，内需政策存在加码的必要性。 7 ⽉末中
央政治局会议要求 “ 实施好更加积极的财政政策和适度宽松的货币政策 ” ，指出要 “ 加⼤逆周期调
节⼒度，加⼒扩⼤内需、优化供给 ” ，下半年关注股市内需叙事的演绎。
内需板块有政策刺激 + 低配共振的潜⼒。本轮⽜市中⻝品饮料、房地产、服务消费等内需板块滞
涨明显，已呈现低股价低配的双低特征。复盘历史经验看，⾏业出现 “ 双低 ” 特征后未来绝对或相
对收益均较为显著，尤其是在下半年内需政策有望加码的背景下，消费、地产板块有望出现修
复。
①⽩酒：在⽩酒渠道库存逐步去化、秋冬旺季备货需求增加的背景下，⽩酒供需格局持续改
善。今年茅台两度逆势提⾼出⼚价，带动批价逐渐企稳回升。未来随着政策提振居⺠收⼊信
⼼，后续茅台批价有望持续趋稳，或推动⾼端⽩酒市场预期跟随回暖，低估低配的⽩酒具备配
置价值。
②地产：随着因城施策的稳地产政策逐渐落实，⽬前部分⼀线城市房价已率先出现企稳态势。
并且近半年以来国内百城房租已逐渐企稳，租⾦或是房价的前瞻指标，后续若房价企稳得到确

认，或带动整体地产市场信⼼回暖，地产板块存在估值修复机会。

### Page 7

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

TUẦN 27-31/07 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX tăng mạnh trong tu ần qua, 49.67 điểm t ương ứng 2.95%, v ới thanh 
khoản suy giảm nhẹ.

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VNINDEX đư ợc dự báo sẽ quay l ại diễn biến giằng co đi ều chỉnh và có th ể thử 
thách về sát hoặc chớm phá vùng đáy cũ một lần nữa trước khi có cơ hội tạo đáy 
vững chắc hơn.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng mạnh trong tuần qua, 49.67 điểm tương ứng 2.95%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm nhẹ. Giấy & lâm sản; Bảo hiểm; Khách sạn, Nhà hàng & Giải trí là các nhóm ngành có 
mức giảm rõ nét nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại có động thái giảm bán ròng so với tuần trước 
với giá trị 2,433 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã HPG, VIC và VNM; trong khi tự doanh mua 
ròng nhẹ ở mức 47 tỷ đồng. 
Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại mua ròng 5,594 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua và đang 
duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 1,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 8,229 hợp đồng nhưng đang 
duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 1,319 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau tuần bán tháo, khi VN-Index bật lên từ vùng đáy quanh 
1.669 điểm và đóng cửa tuần ở 1.735,78 điểm, tăng khoảng 3% so với tuần trước với ba 
phiên tăng liên tiếp giữa tuần. Lực cầu bắt đáy nhập cuộc mạnh hơn khi thị trường bước 
vào cao điểm mùa công bố kết quả kinh doanh quý II, trong đó nhóm ngân hàng và chứng 
khoán với lợi nhuận tăng trưởng tích cực đóng vai trò dẫn dắt đà phục hồi. Áp lực bán 
ròng của khối ngoại tuy vẫn hiện hữu — nổi bật là giao dịch thỏa thuận bán lớn ở phiên 
28/7 — nhưng đã hạ nhiệt dần và thậm chí đảo chiều mua ròng trong phiên 30/7. Ở chiều 
thận trọng, thị trường vẫn dõi theo các yếu tố bên ngoài khi Fed giữ nguyên lãi suất nhưng 
phát thông điệp diều hâu khiến lợi suất trái phiếu Mỹ tăng vọt, cùng diễn biến phức tạp 
của chiến sự tại khu vực Trung Đông ảnh hưởng đến giá dầu. Trong một, hai tuần tới, kết 
quả kinh doanh quý II với sự phân hóa mạnh giữa các nhóm ngành tiếp tục là yếu tố dẫn 
dắt, song nhà đầu tư vẫn cần theo dõi sát diễn biến Trung Đông và động thái của khối 
ngoại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành một cây nến xanh rút chân tích cực trên khung tuần sau khi về sát 
vùng hỗ trợ gần, cho thấy đà tích lũy đã quay trở lại với sự tham gia của lực cầu bắt đáy. 
Trên khung ngày, xu hướng hồi phục tích cực cũng được ghi nhận, tuy nhiên chỉ số bắt đầu 
chịu áp lực điều chỉnh trong phiên cuối tuần khi tiệm cận vùng kháng cự quanh 1760, 
tương ứng đường MA20 đang dốc xuống. Tín hiệu bật tăng trở lại của nhóm bluechips cơ 
bản đang tạo điều kiện hình thành nhịp hồi phục ngắn, tuy nhiên với việc chỉ báo RSI trên 
cả hai khung ngày và tuần đều đã thoát khỏi vùng quá bán và hướng lên vùng trung tính, 
VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ quay lại diễn biến giằng co điều chỉnh và có thể thử thách về sát 
hoặc chớm phá vùng đáy cũ một lần nữa trước khi có cơ hội tạo đáy vững chắc hơn.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1800 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1760 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1695 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1615 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Editorial 
Geopolitics, policy and earnings 
Strategy summary:  The Houthis’ blockade of the Red Sea 
following the resumption of US -Iran hostilities has driven a 
sharp rebound in energy prices and bond yields. We expect US 
electoral constraints and Iranian restraint to eventually limit 
escalation in the conflict. Agai nst this backdrop, central banks 
are likely to hold rates this month as they assess the impact of 
any temporary spike in energy prices on the inflation outlook.  
Besides the spike in bond yields, equity markets will have to 
contend with lofty earnings expectations as the reporting 
season picks up. We expect strong AI investment -driven 
earnings beats, especially in the US and Asia, to drive upgrades 
to profit estimates. This should support continued equity market 
outperformance in the coming months. In fixed income, 
inflation-protected bonds appear particularly attractive, 
especially in the US, where the inflation -adjusted yield has 
surged above 2.4%, close to the highest since 2008.  
Mideast conflict limited by US electoral constraints, Iran 
restraint: The re-escalation of tensions, with Yemen’s Houthis 
joining the conflict, has driven European gas prices back to 
March peaks, but US gasoline prices remain well below the 
latest peaks. Notably, Iran has avoided attacking Saudi Arabia 
and UAE in the latest  escalation, signalling restraint. While 
there is a window for further near-term escalation, any rebound 
in US gasoline prices closer to May’s peaks in the run -up to 
November’s mid -term elections is likely to put pressure on 
President Trump to resume negot iations. Thus, we expect US 
crude oil (WTI) to trade largely in the USD 70 -90/bbl range in 
the next few months, limiting the economic impact.    
Central banks to hold rates this month.  We expect major 
central banks to hold rates this month as they assess the 
inflationary impact of the latest Middle East escalation. This was 
apparent from this week’s ECB decision to hold its deposit rate 
at 2.25%, while it kept the option open for another rate hike later 
this year. Meanwhile, the softer -than-expected US inflation 
report for June confirmed our view that inflation peaked in Q2. 
We expect the Fed to stay on hold this year as long as gasoline 
prices remain subdued, given slowing wage growth and cooling 
shelter and services inflation. As such, the 2-5-year segment of 
the bond yield curve looks increasingly attractive, especially if 
rate expectations peak with oil prices in the next few weeks.  
ECB, BoJ under rising pressure to hike. The ECB, being an 
inflation-targeting central bank, is under growing pressure to 
hike rates in the coming months to counter any energy -price 
driven inflation if European natural gas prices stay elevated. We 
raise the probability of another 25bps rate hike later this year, 
with the timing dependent on the status of the US -Iran conflict 
and the pass-through of energy prices onto inflation and wage 
growth. The BoE is likely to be constrained from hiking rates, 
though, due to weakening domestic growth outlook. Meanwhile, 
the BoJ remains behind the curve as a weaker currency pours 
fuel on domestic wage -driven inflation. There is rising chance 
the BoJ hikes twice this year, if only to support the JPY. 
Equities under rates, earnings scanner. Equities slumped on 
Thursday weighed by a Big Tech earnings report, surging crude 
oil prices (WTI crossed USD 90/bbl and Brent rose above USD 
100/bbl) and the US 10 -year government bond yield rising 
above 4.7%. The 10-year yield has gained almost 35bps since 
29 June. We believe a 25bps rise in bond yields, which serves 
as the discount rate for valuing equities, can impact global 
equities by around 3-4%, so investors should remain disciplined 
as rate expectations shift. However, projected earnings growth 
of 15 -20% in 2026 an d 2027 should provide a meaningful 
cushion against rate-driven valuation pressure. 
US earnings key support for equity outperformance . Big 
Tech is likely to show strong results and clearer AI monetisation 
in Q2. The tech sector has driven over half of global equity EPS 
upgrades since end-February, while AI adoption is still early in 
a multi-year cycle. We remain overweight US technology and 
communication services, and favour regions with stronger 
earnings visibility, particularly the US and Asia ex-Japan.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 24 July 2026

Top client questions 
  What do you expect from US Big Tech earnings next week? 
Our view: We expect US Big Tech companies to deliver solid Q2 
earnings while demonstrating further evidence  of AI 
monetisation. We are Overweight the US technology and 
communication services sectors. 
Rationale: The US technology sector continues to be a primary 
driver of S&P500 earnings, with the highest projected earnings 
growth over the next 12 months. It is expected to see earnings 
growth of 65.6% y/y in Q2 and 57% in 2026, driven by AI demand. 
Besides strong earnings growth, we expect US Big Tech companies 
to demonstrate that elevated AI capex is translating into stronger 
monetisation. US Census Bureau data indicates that AI adoption 
has already reached 20 -25% across US industries. This rapid 
uptake signals strong enterprise acceptance and gives us greater 
confidence that AI usage remains in the early stages of a multi-year 
growth cycle. The emergence of cost-effective and high-performing 
Chinese AI models has raised some concerns about AI 
commoditisation, which  can reduce the returns on AI capex. 
However, we believe that as AI resources become more efficient to 
use, total consumption will rise (Jevons paradox). To gain exposure, 
we are Overweight the US technology and communication services 
sectors, with the latter being dominated by large internet platforms. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
 What is your Q2 earnings outlook for the Japan and Europe 
financial sectors? 
Our view: We expect a constructive earnings season for Japan 
and Europe ex-UK banks and remain Overweight both sectors. 
Rationale: The earnings backdrop for Japan banks is supported by 
the BoJ’s two 25bps rate hikes in December 2025 and Jun e 2026, 
which are feeding through via  lagged loan repricing  and 
expanding domestic net interest margins . Notably, the 
regulatory easing  of Japan banks’ exposure limits  for individual 
corporate groups grants megabanks greater flexibility to underwrite 
large-scale M&A and AI data centre transactions. While the near -
term earnings benefit is incremental, the medium -term implications 
are more meaningful, with greater flexibility to support loan growth.  
Europe banks  are also enter ing the  Q2 earnings season with a 
constructive set of catalysts. Net interest income is benefiting from a 
higher-for-longer rate path, and fee income is supported by resilient 
investment banking activity and a stabilising wealth management 
segment. Meanwhile, credit quality remains robust , with the 
European Banking Authority's June 2026 Risk Assessment showing 
non-performing loan ratios sitting at historic lows. Sustained capital 
distributions via dividends and share buybacks continue to be a key 
catalyst and represent a compelling risk-adjusted return opportunity. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

The technology sector is expected to continue 
leading the US market in earnings growth over the 
next 12 months

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 24 July 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What might be the implications of next week’s  Fed, BoJ 
and BoE policy meetings for bond and FX markets? 
Our view: We expect the Fed, BoJ and BoE to keep policy rates 
unchanged. Guidance will be key: i) a neutral Fed should limit 
further gains in US bond yields and USD, ii) BoJ is expected to 
keep the door open to further policy normalisation; and iii) the 
BoE is likely to retain a cautious inflation bias. 
Rationale: We expect the Fed to hold rates at 3.5-3.75%. Softer 
June US consumer inflation  supports a hold, although sustained 
strength in WTI oil prices could reinforce upside inflation risks. 
A neutral message w ill likely cap short -term gains in government 
bond yields and reduce support for the USD, while renewed 
emphasis on inflation risks would lift yields elevated. The USD Index 
(DXY) is likely to remain rangebound between 100.3 and 102.  
The BoJ is likely to hold its policy rate at 1.00% next week , but 
the weak JPY and imported inflation should preserve the possibility 
of further normalisation. Reports suggest some policymakers see 
scope for faster policy normalisation if JPY weakness, higher fuel 
costs and quicker cost pass -through push inflation above 
projections. Combined with our expectation that the Fed  will remain 
on hold, this should eventually allow USD/JPY to ease. Technically, 
we believe the USD/JPY correction is likely to be gradual rather than 
immediate. FX intervention and higher pension fund allocations 
to domestic assets may slow JPY depreciation, but a sustained 
reversal will require a narrower US-Japan interest rate differential. A 
sustained move below 160 will bring the 155-158 range into focus. 
We expect another hold by BoE on 30 July, following June’s 7-2 vote 
to hold rates at 3.75%. Persistent energy-driven inflation could keep 
UK Gilt yields elevated and support GBP, although weak activity and 
fiscal uncertainty may limit gains. GBP/USD is likely to stay 
rangebound between 1.3200 and 1.3500.   
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
 Will the PBoC implement monetary easing measures , 
given China’s weak Q2 GDP? Will this affect the CNH outlook? 
Our view: A reserve requirement ratio (RRR) cut remains 
possible, but any resulting CNH depreciation should be limited.  
Rationale: China’s economy grew 4.7% in H1, in line with the 
government’s annual growth target, although Q2 GDP moderated to 
4.3% y/y from 5.0%  in Q1 . Industrial production and exports 
remained resilient . Overall macroeconomic conditions do not 
suggest a urgent need for broad-based monetary easing. An RRR 
cut could weigh on the CNH initially by reinforcing concerns over 
wider policy divergence with the US. However, such a cut primarily 
releases banking system liquidity and does not necessarily signal 
a shift towards aggressive monetary easing. We therefore expect 
any CNH weakness to remain  measured, supported by China’s 
external balance and policymakers’ preference for exchange -rate 
stability. USD/CNH has near term support at 6.75. 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

The USD index (DXY) remains rangebound 
between 100.3 and 102

## Document 324: Global events matter, but don't, but do (2026-07-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Global events matter, but don't, but do (2026-07-30).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-07-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

Global events matter,  
but don't, but do

1 
Authors 
Luke Templeman 
Research Analyst

Galina Pozdnyakova 
Research Analyst

July 30, 2026 
Executive summary 
There is a common assumption that markets look through global events such as 
war and geopolitics. But the effects of these and other events extend far more 
broadly into investor portfolios than just the headline indices. Our latest data 
indicates just how much most people oversimplify the reaction of investors and 
markets to global events. 
Indeed, we find that investors do not look through global events. On the contrary, 
they are highly polarised about them. On our analysis, the vast majority of 
investors think global events will either be a “risk” or an “opportunity” for markets. 
That is very different from almost all other broad themes where a higher 
proportion of people simply think there is “no effect” . 
Our dbDataInsights team asked 500 people from the US and UK if and why they 
think global events affect their investment portfolios and what they are doing 
about it. We then used AI to analyse all this unstructured data. In this piece, we 
compare the results to specific datapoints that indicate investors are actively 
reshaping their portfolios to either capture opportunities or hedge the risks that 
they see in global events. 
In the end, we see that positive and negative views on global events can coexist. 
The negative respondents focus on the immediate effects: oil prices rise, inflation 
rises, markets fall. The positive respondents focus on longer-term effects: 
markets adapt, companies innovate, energy systems diversify, and portfolios 
positioned in the right sectors benefit. Both views may be internally coherent; they 
simply operate on different time horizons.

### Page 2

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 2

Why global events are making a comeback 
Global events are more than just war and geopolitics. They extend to incidents 
that affect energy prices, inflation, fiscal sustainability, and technology. Indeed, 
these forces are some of the strongest that affect global economies and we 
discuss them in detail in our piece on global megatrends. 
Our thesis is that , for much of the post -financial-crisis period, low interest rates 
drowned out the other effects of global events. Low rates raised valuations, 
pushed investors into risk assets, and made it easy to explain market moves 
through central bank policy. Now that rates have normalised, global events are 
more visible as market drivers again. 
Look through or not, investors are changing their portfolios 
Our data indicates that investors are reshaping their portfolios as a result of global 
events. These investors view  them not as merely one -off shocks but things that 
are becoming persistent forces.

Figure 1: Over the last three years, have the following thematic megatrends resulted in you making changes to 
your investment portfolio?

Source: Deutsche Bank dbDataInsights

The investor base is therefore not split between people who “understand” global 
events and people who do not. Rather, it is split between investors who emphasise 
different time horizons and different asset exposures. A broad equity investor may 
fear a Middle East shock because it threatens inflation and sentiment. An energy, 
gold, or defence investor may welcome the same shock because their holdings 
could benefit. A long-term diversified investor may view the event as noise.

### Page 3

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 3

Investors are highly polarised about global events 
Retail investors are not passive observers  as many may believe. The following 
table shows that our surveyed investors have strong beliefs both ways  about 
global events and their impact on portfolios. Most importantly, when we look at 
geopolitics, energy prices, and inflation, the proportion of people who think there 
is no effect is very low compared with the other themes.

Figure 2: For each investment theme, is it a risk or an opportunity for investors in 2026? (US)

Source: Deutsche Bank dbDataInsights

Aside from the shock effect, it is understandable that the same global event can 
produce very different portfolio expectations. A war in the Middle East can be 
viewed as a risk because it raises oil prices, hurts consumers, and depresses 
equities. But it c an also be viewed as an opportunity if an investor owns energy 
stocks, gold, defence shares, or believes that markets will rebound after the 
shock. Indeed, in our survey , several positive -impact respondents explicitly 
mentioned these assets and actions. 
Energy is a good example. Short-term energy disruption is painful for consumers 
and companies. Yet, energy disruption can have positive medium -term effects if 
it pushes economies toward greater energy security, diversification of supply, and 
lower energy intensity. Indeed, since 1990 the amount of GDP generated per 
kilogram of oil equivalent has quadrupled in terms of purchasing power parity. In 
other words, economies have become much more energy efficient over time 
which helps explain why the current closure of the Strait of Hormuz has not yet 
had the disastrous effects of the oil shortages during the 1970s.

### Page 4

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 4

This is consistent with our megatrend framework  which shows geopolitics and 
globalisation do not have simple linear impacts on economies. Trade in goods can 
be reorganised through supply chains, and trade in ideas can continue to 
accelerate through connectivity. In other words, geopolitical deterioration is not 
always a pure negative for markets. It changes the winners and losers or increases 
dispersion. 
The most important topics 
The following table shows the top 3 themes that were observed in our survey of 
why people think global events are either positive, negative , or neutral for their 
portfolios.

Figure 3: Top 3 types of global events that matter to people

Source: Deutsche Bank Research

Energy prices are the everyday face of geopolitics 
Energy prices are the mechanism through which many investors experience 
global events most directly. In the written responses, distant conflicts become 
financially relevant because they raise oil, gas, petrol, utilities, shipping costs, 
food prices, and in flation. This is especially visible in the UK negative responses, 
where energy costs and inflation appear repeatedly. 
Energy prices are viewed as a risk by 40% of the US investors surveyed and 55% 
of the UK investors. They are also viewed as an opportunity by 40% of the US 
investors and 29% of UK the investors. The polarisation here is obvious , and the 
country difference matters. The US has a large domestic energy sector, meaning 
some investors may see higher energy prices as positive for certain holdings. The 
UK investor base, by contrast, appears more likely to interpret higher energy 
prices through the household cost-of-living and company-cost channels.

### Page 5

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 5

Why some investors think global events do not matter either 
way 
These investors are not necessarily saying that global events do not matter for 
the world. They are saying global events do not matter enough for their portfolios. 
The main reasons both the US and UK investors in our survey cite are 
diversification, long -term investing, defensive holdings, local exposure, and 
financial discipline. They also frequently think that portfolio outcomes are driven 
more by company fundamentals, dividends, valuations, financial advice, local 
exposure, or long -term compounding, th an geopolitical events. In the written 
responses, this often appears as “markets go up and down,” “over time they 
smooth out,” “my portfolio is diversified,” or “I invest for the long term.” 
There is also a behavioural component. Some investors may choose “no impact” 
because they do not feel confident predicting global events. Several responses in 
the ‘no impact’ columns are uncertain, vague, or non -committal. This does not 
mean respondents believe global events are irrelevant. It may mean they do not 
know how to connect those events to their specific investments. 
The “no impact” camp is therefore divide d into two groups. The first is 
strategically insulated: diversified, long -term, defensive, or locally exposed. The 
second is cognitively uncertain: unsure, disengaged, or unable to explain the link.  
Why some investors think global events will hurt their portfolios 
The ‘negative impact’ responses are dominated by concerns about geopolitics. In 
the US, the most important theme is war and geopolitical conflict, particularly 
Iran, the Middle East, Ukraine/Russia, and escalation risk.  In th e UK , negative 
responses are similar but are even more explicitly tied to the inflationary 
consequences of global events. They frequently mention energy prices, oil, gas, 
the cost of living, and the way foreign conflicts can feed into domestic household 
and business costs. 
When investors are negative about global events, their written responses suggest 
that many see a clear macro chain: 
1. Global conflict disrupts energy, trade, or confidence. 
2. Energy and commodity prices rise. 
3. Inflation and interest-rate pressure increase. 
4. Company margins and consumer spending weaken. 
5. Stock markets fall and portfolio values decline. 
This chain appears again and again in the responses, even when written 
informally. Investors may not use terms such as “supply shock,” “margin 
compression,” or “risk premium,” but they are effectively describing those 
concepts. 
The UK -US difference is notable. Our surveyed US investors appear somewhat 
more willing to see opportunities within global volatility, while the UK investors 
are more likely to view the same themes through the lens of inflation, energy

### Page 6

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 6

dependence, and broader economic vulnerability. That may explain why “World 
Politics and Globalisation cycles” is the highest-ranked cause of portfolio change 
among UK respondents. 
Political uncertainty also has a strong presence in the written responses. In the 
US, this focusses on President Trump, tariffs, foreign policy, and the perceived 
unpredictability in government. In the UK, people also mention Trump and US 
policy, but usually as part of a wider concern about global instability. This implies 
that investors do not separate domestic politics from geopolitics as cleanly as 
analysts often do. Leadership, war, tariffs, inflation, and markets are therefore 
perceived as part of one connected risk cluster. 
Why some investors think global events will help their 
portfolios 
The positive camp contains three broad types of investors. First, the optimist s 
who believe technology and growth will dominate. Second, the sector 
opportunists who own assets that benefit from disruption. Third, the tactical 
buyers who treat volatility as a chance to buy assets cheaply. 
Notably, many investors say they apply a clear investment logic. Essentially, 
‘disruption creates opportunity’. They cite the effect of global events on an 
economic recovery, technology, AI, energy transition, lower prices after market 
sell-offs, defence, oil, gold, emerging markets, and international trade recovery. 
The most important positive theme in the US responses is growth opportunity: 
global events can open new markets, increase demand, create new investment 
opportunities, and raise returns. Technology and AI are also central. Many 
respondents expect AI, digital transformation, health care, renewable energy, and 
innovation-led sectors to do well. 
This US -UK divide appears again in the written responses. US positive 
respondents are frequently enthusiastic about AI, tech, innovation, market 
growth, and sector opportunities. UK respondents are also positive on technology 
and innovation, but their positive responses more often include general optimism, 
economic recovery, and rebounds after conflict. 
There is also an opportunistic trading mindset. Some respondents say market dips 
allow them to buy, or that volatility creates opportunities. 
Indirect effects that are implicit in investor logic 
The most concerning megatrend in the world today is fiscal debt and deficits – 
and it is an important part of the broader story about global events. Fiscal issues 
are less prominent in the written responses from investors compared with war, oil, 
inflation, and President Trump. Yet the implicit themes run through many 
identified factors. Indeed, 3 8% of US and UK respondents view fiscal debt and 
deficits as a risk. At the same time, around a quarter of people in both countries 
view them as an opportunity. 
So, although investors talk frequently about inflation, rates, taxes, government 
borrowing, and weaker growth, they do not always connect those concerns 
explicitly to sovereign deficits. But fiscal anxiety appears indirectly embedded in 
worries about inflation, interest rates, debt, taxes, and government policy.

### Page 7

30 July 2026 
Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank AG Page 7

Government debt and spending has already prompted portfolio changes by 39% 
of the US investors and 29% of the UK investors over the last three years. That is 
lower than technology, world politics, and energy transition , but still significant. 
It suggests that fiscal concerns are not absent, they are simply less emotionally 
vivid than war or energy prices. 
Thus, investors who are positive on AI may be implicitly assuming that technology 
can overpower fiscal and geopolitical headwinds. Investors who are negative on 
global events may be implicitly assuming that debt, conflict, inflation, and policy 
instability will overpower technological progress. The debate is not simply about 
whether global events matter. It is about which global force dominates. 
Conclusion: The polarisation of opinion on global events means 
we should brace for volatility and dispersion 
In the end, the surveyed investors overwhelming think global events matter 
because they reach into their portfolio through inflation, oil, politics, growth, and 
technology. Investors think the world does not matter when they believe their 
portfolio construction, time horizon, or asset selection can neutralise those 
forces. 
The investor base is therefore not split between people who “understand” global 
events and people who do not. Rather, it is split between investors who emphasise 
different time horizons and different asset exposures. 
Cultural differences are important . The surveyed US investors are most likely to 
have changed portfolios because of technology, followed by world politics, while 
the UK investors are most likely to have changed portfolios because of world 
politics and globalisation cycles, and then technology. 
This suggests that US investors are more likely to frame the future around 
innovation and technology  while UK investors see investing more through the 
lens of global political cycles. 
In investment terms, this means global events should be understood less as a 
single directional call on equities and more as a source of dispersion. In other 
words, they just change the relative winners and losers.

### Page 8

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
Global events matter, but don't, but do – Jul 30, 2026

## Document 356: OpenAI内部动荡加剧：重组不断，员工疲惫，高管离职潮持续，上市计划悄然推迟

OpenAI 今年已历经近五次重组，⾼管离职潮持续，⾸席营收官 Denise Dresser 本周成为
最新离职者。公司同时解散了负责评估 AI 模型 “ 灾难性⻛险 ” 的安全团队，引发内部忧虑。尽
管营收增⾄约 400 亿美元，但已被 Anthropic 超越。员⼯普遍感到疲惫， IPO 计划悄然推迟
⾄明年。
摘要：
⼀家估值 8520 亿美元、冲刺万亿 IPO 的公司，正在被⾃⼰的内部混乱拖累。
今年以来， OpenAI 已经历近五次组织重组。⾼管接连出⾛，安全团队被解散，员⼯普遍感到疲
惫。与此同时，原本预期今年完成的 IPO ，已悄然推迟⾄明年。
8 ⽉ 15 ⽇，据英国《⾦融时报》最新报道，多名知情⼈⼠表⽰，反复的⼈事洗牌让部分员⼯对这
家全球最具价值的私⼈科技公司感到 " 沮丧 " 。
⾼管接连出⾛，离职潮未⽌
本周，⾸席营收官 Denise Dresser 宣布离职。她于去年 12 ⽉刚刚上任，负责拓展企业客⼾业
务，任期不⾜⼀年。
这已是近期⼀系列⾼管离职中的最新⼀例。据此前披露，前⾸席财务官兼⾸席运营官 Brad

Lightcap 、伦理主管 Chloé Bakalar 均已离开，任期同样不⾜⼀年。
更早之前，被视为 CEO Sam Altman" ⼆号⼈物 " 的 Fidji Simo ，在病假后于上⽉转为兼职顾问。
但据两名知情⼈⼠透露，她仍⼏乎每天与员⼯保持沟通。⼀名熟悉内情的⼈⼠直⾔： " 她仍在幕
后操控。 " 该⼈⼠还描述，⾼管们正在争夺 Altman 的注意⼒，内部 " 政治 " ⽓氛浓厚。 OpenAI ⽅⾯
则表⽰， Simo 正按顾问职责正常履职。
⼀个值得注意的规律正在浮现：多名⾼管在正式离职前，均被调⼊新设或定义模糊的职位。
Lightcap 在 4 ⽉被转⾄ " 特别项⽬ " 岗位， Kevin Weil 、 Josh Achiam 也经历了类似的过渡安排后相
继离开。
安全团队解散，内部忧虑上升
上⽉底， OpenAI 解散了其 "preparedness" 团队。据多名知情⼈⼠透露，该团队负责评估 AI 模型
是否可能造成严重或灾难性⻛险，并研究应对措施。解散后，相关职责被分散⾄现有团队，由

⾼级员⼯分别负责⽣物安全、⽹络安全等不同领域。
这是 OpenAI 原有研究主导架构下最后⼏块拼图之⼀。此前， "AGI readiness" 和 "mission
alignment" 团队已相继解散。 2024 年，曾联合领导 "superalignment" 团队的 Jan Leike 辞职，并
公开表⽰安全问题正在 " 让位于炫⽬的产品 " 。
preparedness 团队负责⼈ Dylan Scandinaro 已转向研究 " 递归⾃我改进 "AI 的安全影响 —— 即能够
⾃我提升能⼒、训练其他模型的系统。联合创始⼈ Greg Brockman 表⽰， OpenAI 的技术进步需
要 " 更强健 " 的保障措施，公司已做出调整，将研究、安全与安保 " 更深度整合 " 到模型开发中。
然⽽， Bakalar 、 Josh Achiam 以及 Johannes Heidecke 等多名安全领域⼈员的相继离职，加剧
了内部不安。⼀名知情⼈⼠坦⾔： " 这有点令⼈担忧，现在有⼀种紧迫感，必须把这件事做

营收增⻓，但已被 Anthropic 超越
业务层⾯， OpenAI 的年化营收从去年底的 240 亿美元增⾄本⽉约 400 亿美元，增⻓显著。据知情
⼈⼠透露，其中⼤部分增⻓来⾃五周前发布的 GPT-5.6 新模型。
但竞争对⼿ Anthropic 的增速更快。据《⾦融时报》援引知情⼈⼠数据， Anthropic 年化营收从
2025 年底的 90 亿美元，在今年 5 ⽉跃升⾄ 470 亿美元，增幅约五倍，此后仍保持类似增⻓轨迹，

已超越 OpenAI 。
需要指出的是，两家公司均基于近期表现估算全年营收，且收⼊核算⽅式存在差异，直接⽐较
存在⼀定局限。
IPO 推迟，员⼯套现离场
OpenAI 的 IPO 原本预期今年完成，但据知情⼈⼠透露，⽬前已推迟⾄明年，届时估值或⾼达 1 万
亿美元。
与此同时，公司近期完成了近 70 亿美元的股份回购，允许员⼯以 8520 亿美元的估值套现。据现
任及前任员⼯透露，部分⼈套现⾦额超过 1000 万美元。
然⽽，频繁重组带来的代价正在显现。两名知情⼈⼠表⽰，员⼯普遍感到 " 精疲⼒竭 " 和 " 沮丧 " ，
部分⼈将股份回购视为⼀个出⼝。
投资者对此看法不⼀。部分投资者对⾼离职率和公司缺乏专注感到担忧；另⼀些⼈则将此次⼈
事调整定性为上市前的正常梳理。⼀名持有 OpenAI ⼤量股份的投资者表⽰： " 我认为上市前整顿
组织并不罕⻅。你需要清楚⾃⼰的团队是谁。有些离职是健康原因，有些是清理整顿。 "
OpenAI ⽅⾯则表⽰，公司正以 " ⾮凡的速度 " 推进，并 " 不惧怕进⾏组织变⾰ " 。

## Document 361: Rates Spark_ Inflation is and isn’t the issue

Source PDF: all report pdf/Rates Spark_ Inflation is and isn’t the issue.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Article | 12 August 2026 1
THINK economic and financial analysis
Article  |  12 August 2026   RATES SPARK
Rates Spark: Inflation is and isn’t the
issue
US consumer price inflation came in tame, yet bond yields end up where
they were, or higher. The culprit? Higher real yields. The US fiscal deficit
for July subsequently came in high, adding to the pressure. There's a
degree of comfort here at elevated yields, and it's not really all about
inflation. That said, the meandering war backdrop is of no help

The fiscal deficit for July came in on the high. The fiscal deficit for fiscal 2026 is running higher than fiscal
2025
US CPI was good (as expected), while the fiscal deficit was bad
(also as [we] expected)
There was no big market reaction to the benign CPI report for July. Market break-evens have
been discounting a benign inflation story for some time now, with break-evens comfortably
below the 2.5% printed for core inflation. Still, it was a good number, and better for the front

end than the back end on the theory that it takes pressure off the rate hike narrative. That
said, higher real yields were the chief underpinning on the day, and that's in fact been
thematic over the past number of months. The thing is, real yields today are not high. They are
normal. Back to where they were pre-GFC. Which suggests they can remain elevated.
The US fiscal deficit for July came in at USD432bn – high! The cumulative fiscal deficit is now

### Page 2

THINK economic and financial analysis
Article | 12 August 2026 2
running at USD1.8tr. That's some USD170bn higher vs 2025. It's now homing in on coming in
some USD200bn higher than for fiscal year 2025. Not a number that bonds typically get
excited by, as for bonds, it's really all about the issuance number and profile. But this does
place upside to issuance pressure ahead, which should add some credit pressure to the
determination of long-end rates. The deficit had been shielded by tariff income. Tariff refunds

are part of the issue. But the underlying picture is tending to turn net sour also.
The overall prognosis here is for the 10yr Treasury yield to trend towards the 4.75% to 5%
area. Going above is not an option that Treasury Secretary Bessent would accept. But getting
close is the trade.
Count down to the reopening of the SSA primary market
The public sector EUR primary market is currently still in its summer break. Although we are
seeing some issuers on the government side maintain their activity, others, like Italy or Spain,

have cancelled upcoming bond auctions for August.
In the broader EUR-denominated Sovereign, Supranational and Agency (SSA) space, the last
benchmark deal dates back to early July, and there has been a drought since mid-July. Last
year, it was the German issuers, one Lander issuer followed by KFW, who were first to come
out after the summer break around this time of the year – i.e. mid-August. We have now seen
IBB (Investitions Bank Berlin) mandate a bank for a €0.5bn social bond tap, but a first EUR SSA

benchmark deal should come soon after, with next week likely to see the primary market
reopening in full swing. That said, many issuers are entering the second half of the yearly
issuance cycle with their funding progress already well advanced.

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
SpaceX 正式完成对⼈⼯智能编程初创公司 Cursor 的收购，交易⾦额⾼达 600 亿美元，成为
科技⾏业史上规模最⼤的并购案之⼀，标志着⻢斯克在 AI 领域与 Anthropic 及 OpenAI 的竞

SpaceX 庞⼤的先进 AI 芯⽚资源。
摘要：
SpaceX 正式完成对⼈⼯智能编程初创公司 Cursor 的收购，交易⾦额⾼达 600 亿美元，成为科技
⾏业史上规模最⼤的并购案之⼀，标志着⻢斯克在 AI 领域与 Anthropic 及 OpenAI 的竞争进⼊新阶
段。
据监管⽂件显⽰，此次收购于 8 ⽉ 14 ⽇正式⽣效，距 SpaceX 宣布达成收购协议仅约两个⽉。这
笔交易旨在帮助⻢斯克旗下现已更名为 SpaceXAI 的 AI 业务补强软件能⼒，切⼊以编程为核⼼的

AI ⼯具市场 —— 这⼀市场被视为 AI 商业化最具价值的赛道之⼀。
收购完成后，Cursor 联合创始⼈将跻⾝亿万富翁⾏列，其团队亦将获得 SpaceX 庞⼤的先进 AI 芯
⽚资源。Cursor 在⼀篇博客⽂章中表⽰， " 我们将能够使⽤全球规模最⼤的 GPU 集群，这将为我
们提供算⼒，以构建更强⼤、同时运⾏成本更低的模型。 "
Cursor ：从⾼速成⻓到并⼊ SpaceX 版图
Cursor 的 AI 编程助⼿于 2023 年推出，旨在帮助程序员更⾼效地编写和调试代码。
凭借软件开发者对 " 提⽰式编程 " （ vibe coding ）⼯具的强劲需求， Cursor 迅速成为增⻓最快的
初创公司之⼀，并成为这⼀技术浪潮的核⼼玩家。
此次并购使 Cursor 团队得以接⼊ SpaceX 的算⼒基础设施，为其开发更具竞争⼒的模型提供⽀
撑。在交易正式完成之前， SpaceX 与 Cursor 已于今年 7 ⽉联合推出⾸个合作模型 Grok 4.5 ，该
模型聚焦编程、⾦融及法律任务，据两家公司介绍，其使⽤成本低于其他竞争模型。此后，

SpaceX ⼜相继发布了 Grok Bot—— ⼀款可像 AI 代理团队⼀样全天候处理任务的产品 —— 以及升
级版 Grok 4.6 模型。
⻢斯克押注软件： AI 收⼊或超 SpaceX 其他业务总和
尽管 SpaceX 已与 Anthropic 、 Alphabet 旗下⾕歌等公司签署多项价值数⼗亿美元的协议，向外部
企业开放其算⼒资源，但⻢斯克显然将软件能⼒视为更为关键的战略⽅向。
据悉，⻢斯克在 8 ⽉与 SpaceX 员⼯的⼀次内部会议上明确表⽰，公司 " 必须在软件层⾯取得成
功 " ，并强调⼈⼯智能是公司的当下与未来。他在会议上称， " 我们的 AI 收⼊将在 9 ⽉超过
SpaceX 所有其他业务的收⼊总和。 "
SpaceXAI 此前在企业市场的渗透相对有限，并经历了⼀系列裁员与重组。收购 Cursor 被视为该
业务扭转局⾯、加速商业化落地的关键举措，也是⻢斯克在 AI 军备竞赛中追赶 OpenAI 与
Anthropic 的重要⼀步。

## Document 397: “AI版金融创新”：CDO vs CCO=2008 vs 2026？

Source PDF: all report pdf/“AI版金融创新”：CDO vs CCO=2008 vs 2026？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-08-15
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

当前 AI 繁荣本质并⾮科技周期，⽽是由 “ 算⼒即抵押品 ” （ CCO ）驱动的信⽤与地产周期。英
伟达与私营巨头搭建 5000 亿美元融资平台，将⻛险证券化分发。在超⼤规模云⼚商资本开
⽀增速拐点（⼆阶导数）已现、 OpenAI 等关键借款⼈过度依赖再融资的背景下，这套⾼度
同构于 2008 年次贷危机的信⽤架构极易在增⻓放缓时触发断裂。
摘要：
当市场还在争论 AI 是否重演 2000 年科技泡沫时，⼀套与 2008 年次贷危机⾼度同构的信⽤架构，
已悄然搭建完毕。
本周，英伟达相继与 Apollo 、⻉莱德、⿊⽯、 Brookfield 、⾼盛及 KKR 签署谅解备忘录，计划联
合搭建 " 算⼒融资平台 " ，旨在动员逾 5000 亿美元第三⽅资本。其核⼼逻辑是： GPU 芯⽚可装⼊
特殊⽬的载体、作为抵押品，并以其预期产⽣的现⾦流为基础进⾏融资 —— 这与次贷危机时期
楼宇、收费公路的融资模式如出⼀辙。
与此同时， CoreWeave 已于 2026 年 5 ⽉完成⾸只公开银团 GPU 抵押融资⼯具 DDTL 5.0 ，⾦额 31

亿美元，底层借款⽅包括 OpenAI 和 Cohere 。 " 抵押算⼒凭证 " （ Collateralized Compute
Obligations ， CCO ）正式登场。
信⽤市场已在对暴跌的 Token 成本定价；股票市场却仍沉浸在算⼒叙事的亢奋之中。这⼀裂⼝，
与 2006 年次贷违约率开始上⾏、⽽房价依然创历史新⾼时如出⼀辙 —— 彼时，市场误以为价格
下跌才是危机的起点，却忽略了真正的扳机：增速的拐点，⽽⾮增速本⾝归零。
核⼼误判： AI 是科技周期，还是信⽤周期？
据 Groundbreaker ⽹站今年 7 ⽉发布的分析，当前 AI 繁荣的本质并⾮科技创新周期，⽽是⼀个由
信⽤驱动、结构上⾼度类似商业地产的资本形成周期。两者在断裂逻辑上截然不同：科技周期
死于产品⽆⼈问津或竞争淘汰，可以缓慢去估值；信⽤和地产周期死于需求增速的放缓 —— 不
需要需求崩塌，只需增⻓停⽌加速，结构便已断裂。
这正是 " ⼆阶导数 " 的要义。设某核⼼变量为 S ，其⽔平值（ S 本⾝）和⼀阶导数（增速）是市场

唯⼀盯盘的数字；⽽⼆阶导数 —— 增速本⾝是否还在加速 —— ⼏乎⽆⼈建仓于此。然⽽，任何
将增速持续加速作为内嵌前提的融资结构，恰恰死在⼆阶导数的拐点上，⽽⾮⼀阶导数归零之
时。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

从实际数据看，超⼤规模云⼚商（ Hyperscaler ）资本开⽀占运营现⾦流的⽐率已从 2022 年的约
30% 升⾄ 2025 年的约 60% ， 2026 年按共识预期将触及 100%—— 此后每⼀个边际算⼒投⼊，都
必须依赖债务或股权融资。资本开⽀总量的加速度（⼆阶导数）已于 2025 年⻅顶并转负：增速
从约 +81% 逐步收窄，加速度已由约 +30 个百分点转为约 -25 个百分点。⽔平值仍在创历史新⾼，
速度依然为正，但加速度已经反转。
算⼒即抵押品： CCO 的结构解析
英伟达本周的动作，将上述架构的规模放⼤⾄ 5000 亿美元。⻩仁勋已明⾔：科技芯⽚已成为抵
押品。英伟达本⾝为部分交易提供最⾼ 25% 的剩余价值担保 —— 与杠杆租赁中的剩余价值保证
如出⼀辙。
这套架构的每⼀个特征，都是地产开发的算⼒翻版：数据中⼼是实物抵押品，按⽤必付（ take-
or-pay ）合同是租约，合同所代表的剩余履约义务（ RPO ）是贷款账本，资本开⽀是放贷本
⾦，算⼒使⽤费是债务偿付。当媒体将超⼤规模云⼚商创纪录的资本开⽀解读为 " 需求信⼼ " 时，

其实读到的是放贷规模 —— 市场在庆祝贷款增⻓，却把它叫作营收增⻓。
" 发起 - 分销 " 机制正在完成闭环。 CoreWeave 的 DDTL 5.0 是第⼀只公开银团化 GPU 抵押⼯具，设
有破产隔离融资⼦公司，已进⼊⼆级市场流通。这意味着，与 OpenAI 信⽤质量挂钩的⻛险已离
开原始持有⼈的资产负债表，扩散⾄更⼴泛的信⽤市场 —— 这与 2005 ⾄ 2007 年 CDO 的分发路径
⾼度重合。
" 裸露借款⼈ " ： OpenAI 的信⽤解剖
每⼀个次贷周期都有⼀个只能以再融资偿还旧债、永⽆法真正还清本⾦的借款⼈。在本轮周期
中，该⻆⾊由 OpenAI 担任。
据 Groundbreaker 分析， OpenAI 的收⼊结构约 60% 来⾃消费端，较企业端更为脆弱；其烧钱率
约占营收的 57% ，累计现⾦消耗预计到 2029 年将逼近 1150 亿美元，本⼗年内⽆望实现正现⾦
流。其偿付能⼒不依赖盈利，⽽依赖于每⼀轮估值⽐上⼀轮更⾼ —— 估值增值本⾝就是现⾦
流。
从融资步伐看， OpenAI 的轮次估值从 2024 年初约 860 亿美元起步，历经约 1570 亿、 3000 亿、

5000 亿，到 2026 年春约达 8520 亿美元；对应的轮次步进倍数依次为 1.83× 、 1.91× 、 1.67× 、
1.70× ，⽽隐含 IPO 步进倍数仅约 1.23×—— 既是序列中最低值，也是最终必须向公开市场实际兑
现的那⼀关。 IPO 延期，已是这⼀算术逻辑的直接结果。

### Page 3

OpenAI 没有真正的联署担保⼈。微软于 2026 年 4 ⽉已撤除全部结构性⽀撑 —— 终⽌收⼊分成、
放弃独家权、放弃优先算⼒供应权，仅保留 27% 股权上⾏收益； SoftBank 正试图以 OpenAI 持股
为抵押筹集 100 亿美元保证⾦贷款，且需提供个⼈担保，显⽰连联署⼈本⾝也需要联署⼈。在消
息最灵通的交易对⼿微软看来， OpenAI 的信⽤已不值承销 —— 其⾏为本⾝即为信号。
相⽐之下， Anthropic 的信⽤结构截然不同：约 80% 营收来⾃企业端，每 1 美元算⼒对应 1.70 美元
营收，且拥有⾕歌和博通为约 350 亿美元 TPU 融资设施提供的实质性信⽤背书，信⽤质量被合成
提升⾄投资级。同为前沿⼤模型公司，两者之间是 " 有担保信⽤ " 与 " 裸露借款⼈ " 的本质差异。
2.1 万亿美元账单：集中度⻛险与相关性陷阱
据 Groundbreaker 估算，四⼤超⼤规模云平台合计 RPO 约 2.1 万亿美元，其中约⼀半 —— 约 1.05
万亿 —— 来⾃ OpenAI 和 Anthropic 。微软账本中这两家合计约占 49% ； Oracle 约占 54% ，仅

OpenAI ⼀家即约达 3000 亿美元；⾕歌约 43% ；亚⻢逊约 51% 。
这正是 2008 年⾼级别 CDO 分级致命的结构性根源：表⾯上分散的数千笔抵押贷款，实则共同暴
露于同⼀个宏观变量 —— 全国房价⾛势；当这⼀变量转向，所有资产的相关性瞬间从零变为
⼀。本轮周期中，那个宏观变量不是房价，⽽是 OpenAI 能否完成下⼀轮融资。据报道，芯⽚股
在 OpenAI 传出 IPO 可能从 2026 年推迟⾄ 2027 年时即告下跌，印证了这条传导链的存在。
CoreWeave 的 DDTL 5.0 正在将这种集中的、与 OpenAI 信⽤质量⾼度相关的⻛险证券化并向市
场分发。该⼯具的偿付并⾮来⾃ OpenAI 的盈利，⽽是来⾃ OpenAI 持续融资的能⼒ —— ⽽后者的
基础是 AI 资本开⽀叙事的持续强化。

### Page 4

纳什均衡的反转：谁先眨眼？
资本开⽀军备竞赛是⼀种纳什均衡，但有条件：它成⽴的前提是市场持续奖励每⼀个新增算⼒
投⼊，将其定价为增⻓期权。⼀旦某家超⼤规模云⼚商宣布削减资本开⽀⽽股价不跌反涨 ——
纪律被奖励⽽⾮惩罚 —— 整个博弈矩阵即告重写：⽀出从占优策略变为被惩罚的策略，克制从
⽰弱变为重新定价的信号。这⼀均衡的反转不是渐进的，⽽是协调性的突变，第⼀个被奖励的
削减者，将同时给所有其他 CFO 提供掩护和激励。
⾼盛 Delta One 交易主管曾直⾔：
从各家压⼒测试来看，⾕歌凭借 TPU 结构性成本优势及超过 4600 亿美元云业务积压，在竞争对
⼿收缩时受益，不会先动； Oracle 资本开⽀约占销售额 86% ，资产负债表已⾼度拉伸，其压⼒
将以信⽤事件形式显现⽽⾮主动选择；亚⻢逊⾃由现⾦流已转负，可能被资本市场倒逼；Meta
的扎克伯格持有双重表决权股份，且旗下⽆公有云业务对外销售算⼒，资本开⽀防御最弱、退
出最易，最有可能成为第⼀个眨眼者。
断裂序列：机制⽽⾮⽇历
Groundbreaker 勾勒出⼀条明确的断裂传导链：

" 第⼀家发出信号、可以放缓⽀出步伐的超⼤规模云⼚商，其股价可能将获奖励（并重创半导体
股）。如果发⽣这种情况，其他家会跟进。这才是最终令资本开⽀周期停滞的反⾝性 —— 不是需
求缺失，⽽是投资者判定下⼀美元⽀出的边际回报已不再具有吸引⼒。 "
（ 1 ）  终局再融资⽆法以所需步进倍数完成定价 ——OpenAI IPO 延期是信号；
（ 2 ）  OpenAI 削减算⼒承诺以保存现⾦，触发按⽤必付合同违约；
（ 3 ）  新兴算⼒云平台（ neocloud ）⾸当其冲 ——CoreWeave 、 Lambda 等本质上是 " 利差交
易 " ，没有第⼆条现⾦流，利差倒挂即意味着结构性资不抵债； Oracle 承受集中度冲击，但以减
值⽽⾮⽌付形式显现；
（ 4 ）  信⽤市场冻结： RPO 从远期需求重新定价为对⼿⽅⻛险， GPU 抵押票据⽆法展期， " 发起 -
分销 " 机制卡住；

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

3 条评论
值得注意的是，分析本⾝承认时间窗⼝存在不确定性 —— 私募市场新⼀轮⼤额融资、主权或战
略性资本的兜底介⼊、以及超⼤规模云⼚商借助⾃⾝账本持续加杠杆，均可延⻓ " 借来的时间 " 。
但据摩根⼠丹利测算，投资级超⼤规模云⼚商的总债务杠杆已在⼀年内翻倍⾄约 1.8 倍，⾼于整
个能源板块，⽽表外⻋辆中的千亿美元规模尚未计⼊。评级机构的空间已所剩不多。
历史回响：三重错误的叠加
Groundbreaker 的论断以三重并列错误收尾，每⼀重单独存在或可消化，三者叠加则精确复制了
上⼀次重⼤信⽤事件的前提条件：
其⼀，市场将 AI 定性为科技周期，⽽其融资机制是信⽤与地产周期的机器；其⼆，市场盯住⽔
平值与速度，⽽结构断裂于加速度；其三，市场将⾼度集中、单⼀借款⼈主导的贷款账本，当

作分散化的远期需求在定价。
2008 年的教训从未被准确记忆。违约不是在价格下跌时开始的，⽽是在价格停⽌更快上涨时开
始的 —— 正如 2006 年次贷违约率在房价仍创新⾼时悄然上⾏。这⼀次，这套融资架构被设计得
极为精密，⽽它恰好断裂于那个⽆⼈盯盘的数字：增速的加速度。
（ 5 ）  权益市场遭受重创 —— 资本开⽀从期权重新定价为成本，整条产业链估值压缩；
（ 6 ）  资本最充裕的参与者以低价收购滞压数据中⼼，承接必须延续的租约。

上天氣過熱的降溫需求 ，及提前補氣過冬需求 ，預
期4Q26 TTF價格仍有上行風險，才能避免美國LNG
轉往亞洲 ，但若荷姆茲海峽運力回升速度快於預期 ，
TTF價格將回落。
重點摘要
• 天然氣：美國天然氣庫存增
加，預期隨管道瓶頸改善使
4Q26美國天然氣價格持平於
去年同期；歐洲因庫存偏低
的回補需求將使天然氣價格
偏上行。
• 成品油供應緊張 ，柴油裂解
價差保持強勁 ，有利煉油商 。
• 預期綜合油企 3Q26獲利略低
於2Q26，但獲利年比仍成長 ，
維持股東回報 ，但煉油商獲
利上升，較看好煉油商。
下圖/煉油廠停產數量高於過去
資料來源：高盛，2026/7/31，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/8/10
2
《成品油供應緊張，有利維持偏高的煉油利潤》
受煉油廠關廠 ，產能因戰事受損 ，成品油供應比原
油更緊張，估計全球煉油廠開工量年減 700萬桶/日，
遠高於過去區間 ，在供應減少下 ，柴油利潤保持強
勁，廠商宣布檢修延期 ，減少汽油產量 ，轉利潤較

高的柴油 ，有利美國煉油廠維持偏高的利潤 ，獲利
成長，其下行風險在於川普要求煉油商降價。
《3Q26能源類股較看好煉油廠》
美綜合油企3Q26受上游較低的產量及原油均價影響，
但被成品油供應緊張及高利潤 ，檢修延期，業務受
惠柴油高裂解價差及 LNG出口量增加抵銷 ，獲利恐
略低於2Q26，但年比仍大幅成長，然若油價上揚，
獲利將優於預期 ，現金流仍強大 ，維持資本紀律及
股東回報。然3Q26煉油商獲利上升，較看好煉油商。

格轉嫁成本上揚 ，塗料端，宣偉受惠成長投資 、新
客戶及市占率提升 ，所有業務除 DIY塗料下滑外 ，
都呈現成長，3Q26消費品牌呈高雙位數成長，其餘
中個位數成長 ，預期3Q26獲利YOY+3%；藝康受
惠定價上漲 ，下半年提高能源附加費漲幅 ，抵銷中
東動盪，利潤率提升 ，預期獲利YOY+5%。化學氣
體林德2Q26受惠銷量 、價格/產品組合及匯率分別

貢獻2%營收成長率，產業以電子及製造業貢獻成長
較多，但美國家庭護理受成本壓力，對3Q26展望持
平2Q26，獲利YOY約7~8%，未符合市場上調預期 。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/8/7 ，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/8/10

原料上漲壓力 ，利潤下降 。

《美鋼廠獲利成長；受供給增使2H26鐵礦砂價轉弱》
受美國國內供給量有限 ，需求穩健 、庫存偏低及美
國鋼廠漲價 ，今年美國進口鋼材量逐季增加 ，進口
市占率預估七月提高1%至18%，累積七月市占率約
16%，境內開工率約 81%，已在過去循環相對高點 ，
關稅政策沒有變化 ，預期鋼價維持高檔 ，美鋼廠受
惠鋼廠與原物料成長，財報優於預期，預期3Q26受

惠製造回流 、進口替代 、鋼廠和鋼鐵產品價格的上
漲，獲利將優於2Q26。中國重點城市推出放寬買房
限制、買房補貼和提高貸款成數等政策 ，使中國百
大房企今年前七月銷售同比降幅較前六月收窄 ，今
年上半年製造業 、基礎設施投資同比也下降 ，六月
中國粗鋼與鋼材產量持平 ，將加快新增專項債發行
速度，以抵銷地方政府因債務壓力而緊縮的基礎設
施投資，但房地產焦點在托底而非強刺激 ，七月製

《預期金價短期承壓，但中期仍看升》
近月金價歷經美伊互相衝突推升實質利率而跌破
4000美元，但伊朗與阿曼談判荷姆茲海峽重開安排 、
美就業降溫 ，使升息預期降溫 ，加上川普重啟罷免
庫克程序 ，FED獨立性受考驗及中國人行加大購金
托底，金價反彈至4,400美元以上。但美伊互提賠償
條件，雙方和解難度升 ，加上油價若恢復上漲 ，恐
推動升息預期 ，為短線金價上檔壓力 ，金價仍有回
跌可能，但本行認為下半年升息機率低 ，若美伊衝
突帶來的不利因素漸消除 ，FED轉鴿派或市場縮減
升息轉降息預期；受擔憂債務可持續性 、分散交易
對手風險及多元化資產配置 ，據45%的受訪央行仍
認為將未來一年內增加黃金儲備 ，高於去年調查 ，

年底可望進入黃金需求旺季，維持金價中期上行。

## Document 484: 从“拖地”到“享地”：中国家庭拖把使用场景升级白皮书

### Page 3

3©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
摘要
场景重构 - 从功能满足到情感价值的痛点转化
中国家庭的清洁场景已从日常维护、周末深度、突发状况到婴宠特需全面升级， 消费者痛点从“能否清洁干净”跃迁至对“省时省力、无痕无残留、
操作便捷、健康安全”的情感价值诉求，驱动清洁工具从基础功能满足走向情感与健康满足的深度融合。
未来展望 – “享地”时代的清洁新图景
未来清洁市场正迈向“轻松体验、美学表达、健康守护”三位一体的新图景，宝家洁以材料科学颠覆传统胶棉拖把品类，引领从“把地拖干净”到
“享受清洁过程”的全面跃迁。
人群洞察 - 六大典型用户画像
中国家庭清洁消费者市场分为效率至上、品质苛求、银发务实、租房过渡、新中产美学、宠物主理六大人群，分别驱动着清洁工具向省时省力、完美
无痕、轻便免洗、快速高效、美学仪式、防缠无死角等多元方向精细化升级。效率至上型追求“省时高效”，拒绝中途洗拖把、缠毛发、返工；品质苛求
型视清洁为生活仪式，愿为耐用工艺和高清效果付费；银发务实型拒绝复杂功能，只要轻便、不弯腰、能进角落、价格实在；租房过渡型追求“轻便实用、

快速搞定”；新中产美学型要求工具高颜值、能养护地面、散发香味，清洁即生活美学；宠物主理型追求防缠无死角、除菌净味。
洁净觉醒 - 中国家庭清洁意识变迁
中国家庭清洁正经历从“功能满足”到“体验满足”再到“情感与健康满足”的跨越式跃迁。 中国家庭的清洁观念经历了从物质匮乏年代的 “凑合”
到如今“精致健康”的跨越式升级；户型变革重塑中国家庭的清洁场景，清洁工具正从 “通用拖把”走向“户型适配+场景切换+效率优先”的迭代方向；
清洁工具也随之从“能拖地”向“拖得享受”迭代。
决策解码 - 消费者购买行为分析
消费者选购清洁用品以理性务实为主导（效果、省力、耐用、性价比），但健康环保等附加值的付费意愿正形成重要增长力量；代际差异显著（ 00后
重悦己、80/90后重均衡、70后及以上重实惠），信息获取以短视频和电商为主，清洁工具使用痛点聚焦毛发缠绕与清洁过程中的重复劳动、频繁清洗。

### Page 4

4
洁净觉醒-中国家庭清洁意识变迁01

### Page 5

中国家庭清洁正处在从“功能满足”向“情感与健康满足”的跨越期
受社会经济进步、居住空间升级、健康意识觉醒以及产品技术迭代的驱动，中国家庭清洁意识正在经历从 “功能满足”到
“体验满足”再到“情感与健康满足”的跃迁。中国家庭清洁正处在从 “生存刚需”进化为“品质生活”的过程中。

人们健康意识觉醒，清洁目

和“悦己”，清洁行为从

### Page 6

6©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
来源 ：艾瑞消 费研究 院根据公 开资料整 理及绘 制
理念升级：从"干净"到"健康"的代际变迁
中国家庭的清洁观念经历了从物质匮乏年代的 "凑合"，到如今"精致健康"的跨越式升级。不同代际群体对于"什么是干净"的定
义，正在发生深刻裂变。
40/50后（65岁以上）
"能用就行"的实用主义
• 群体特点：对于40后、50后而
言，拖把的终极使命就是 "把地
弄湿"。他们的清洁标准是 看得
过去就行。一块旧毛巾、一根布
条拖把，能用十几年不换。
60/70后（47-65岁）
"肉眼无尘"的初代品质追求
• 群体特点：60后、70后是“改
革开放”的同龄人，他们见证了
中国家庭从"生存"到"生活"的转
变。他们干净的终极标准是 "肉
眼看不到灰尘"——地砖能反光、
桌面没水渍，就足够了。
80/90后（27-46岁）
"细菌螨虫零容忍"的健康焦虑
• 群体特点：80后、90后成长于
信息爆炸时代， "看不见的脏才

是真的脏"——这个观念深入骨
髓。地面上的细菌、螨虫、过敏
原，才是他们真正恐惧的东西。
后疫情时代更是强化了这一倾向。
00后（18-26岁）
"清洁即疗愈"的新消费哲学
• 群体特点：00后正在颠覆"做家
务"的定义——拖地不再是苦差
事，而是解压仪式 。他们的清洁
哲学不是"清洁是为了干净"，而
是"清洁本身就是一种生活方式 "。
对这个群体而言，拖把不只是工
具，而是生活美学的组成部分。
• 产品诉求：这一代人的清洁工具
更换周期普遍较长 ，对"免手洗"、
"抗菌"等概念接受度低，但对 价
格极度敏感。
• 产品诉求：他们是第一批 "家务
工具升级"的践行者。他们推动
了旋转拖把、胶棉拖把的普及。
他们升级逻辑是 "功能优先"：能
甩干就行，能不弯腰更好，对颜
值、香氛需求低。 "耐用"、"实
用"仍是核心诉求。
• 产品诉求：80后、90后是当前
拖把"品质升级"的主力买单人 。
他们愿意为"除菌率99%"等功
能标签支付溢价。对 "成分安全"
高度敏感：无磷、无荧光剂、可
降解成为选购关键词。

• 产品诉求：他们的清洁哲学是
“愉悦驱动”而非“任务驱动，
享受“拖地” 本身。清洁工具
必须好看、好用——拖地要有
仪式感；必须“不委屈” ——
不能脏手、弯腰、累 。香氛、色
彩成为重要加分项。

7©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
来源 ：艾瑞消 费研究 院根据公 开资料整 理及绘 制
空间升级：户型变迁重塑清洁场景
小户型（<60m2）
"螺蛳壳里做道场"的收纳战争
小户型是中国城市青年的 “刚需基本款”。公开数据
显示， 2025年上半年60㎡以下小户型在一线城市的成交
量同比增长15%。
户型特点
• 清洁面积虽小，但杂物多、死角多；
• 收纳空间极度稀缺，拖把必须 “不占地”；
• 厨房、卫生间、客厅功能高度叠加，清洁需要频繁切换；
• 租房比例高，搬家频繁，拖把需要 “轻便好带走”。
工具诉求
• 需要“全能型”拖把，一把顶三把 ：能拖地、能擦台面、
能处理卫生间积水，还能轻松收纳。
改善型需求爆发，大户型成交占比持续攀升。 截至2026
年5月，全国120 ㎡以上大户型在新房成交中的占比为 46%。
户型特点
• 多材质地面并存：客厅木地板、卫生间瓷砖、阳台仿古砖；
• 清洁面积大，效率成为核心诉求；
• 家政服务使用频率高，但日常维护仍需自主完成；

• 分区清洁意识强：厨卫、客厅、卧室需要不同清洁策略。
工具诉求
• 为效率买单： 宽面板、长续航、多功能的拖把是刚需 。
• 全地面适配成为关键需求 ：一把拖把搞定木地板、瓷砖、
大理石，不用换来换去。
开放式厨房正在席卷中国家庭。据公开数据， 50%的
中国家庭厨房形态为开放式 /开敞式，未来选择开放式厨房
的比例或将升至65%。
户型特点
• 厨房油烟、客厅灰尘、餐厅污渍 "三位一体"；
• 清洁频次被迫提升，日常维护压力增大；
• 厨房重油、客厅日常、卫生间除菌的分区清洁需求凸显；
• 对"清洁效率"要求更高：一次清洁要覆盖多个功能区。
工具诉求
• 需要工具具备 “多场景切换”能力，场景化、专业化的
拖把组合成为产品机会 ：理想状态下，一套拖把系统能
覆盖厨房吸水去油、客厅日常维护、卫生间除菌干燥。
大户型（>120m2） 开放式空间
户型变革正在重塑中国家庭的清洁场景，并倒逼清洁工具进行精准创新。清洁工具正从 "通用拖把"走向"户型适配+场景切换
+效率优先"的迭代方向——小户型求集成，大户型求高效，开放空间求专业。

大house的幸福与忧伤 厨房-客厅-餐厅一体化的清洁噩梦

### Page 8

8©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
来源 ：艾瑞消 费研究 院根据公 开资料整 理及绘 制
工具进化：家庭清洁拖把进入3.0时代——拖得享受
中国家庭拖把的演变史，是一部浓缩的消费升级简史。从 "能拖地"到"拖得干净"，再到"拖得享受"，这个品类正在经历第三次
革命。
改革开放初期， 中国家庭的拖把几乎是 “手边有什么就用什么 ”。旧衣服撕成的布条、工厂淘汰的棉纱是这个时代
的主流。“拖把”这个词甚至还没普及，农村叫 “墩布”，城市叫“拖布”，五花八门。1.0时代的拖把，本质上是
“消耗品”而非“工具”。消费者对它的期待只有两个字：不坏。
• 代表产品：布条拖把、棉纱拖把
• 技术特点：纯物理摩擦清洁，无任何技术含量
关键词：布条拖把、棉纱拖把、能用就行
1.0时代
（1980年-2000年）
功能启蒙，传统工具主导
进入21世纪，“便利”成为拖把升级的核心词 。旋转拖把的离心甩干原理，让 消费者告别了"手拧脏水"的时代；胶
棉拖把的超强吸水性，让卫生间积水不再是难题。但 "拖什么"、"拖完怎么样"的问题依然存在。胶棉头易发硬、拖后留

水印、毛发缠绕 ……这些痛点，2.0时代的工具只解决了一半。
• 代表产品：旋转拖把、胶棉拖把、平板拖把
• 技术特点：离心甩干、吸水材料升级、拖把头可换洗
关键词：旋转拖把、胶棉拖把、平板拖把、脱水方便
2.0时代
（2000年-2015年）
功能升级，便利性提升
当“健康中国”战略遇上“Z世代消费哲学 ”，拖把行业被推入了 3.0时代。这个时代的竞争焦点，不是 “能不能拖
干净”，而是“拖地是不是一种享受 ”。而核心技术 ——欧科棉的出现，让拖把 可以365天随拿随用，消费者不用等待、
不用忍受"硬邦邦"的触感。
• 代表产品：静电除尘拖把、蒸汽拖把、喷雾拖把、欧科棉拖把
• 技术特点：多功能合一、健康杀菌、材质升级（超细纤维 /欧科棉）、设计美学化
关键词：静电除尘 拖把、蒸汽杀菌、 喷雾拖把、 欧科棉拖把、 "享地"体验
3.0时代
（2015年至今）
体验革命，情感价值崛起
中
国
家
庭
拖
把
演
变

### Page 9

9
人群洞察-六大典型用户画像02

### Page 10

10©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
来源 ：N=500，于2026年5月通 过艾瑞智 研平台-消费 洞察数据 库调研 获得
年龄、收入、住房情况、家庭结构等背景因素影响了人们对家庭清洁的行为、诉求和期待。所有人都期待“无痕”“免手
洗”“防缠绕”“无死角” ，而差异点体现在效率至上型追求“省时高效”，拒绝中途洗拖把、缠毛发、返工；品质苛求型
视清洁为生活仪式，愿为耐用工艺和高清效果付费；银发务实型拒绝复杂功能，只要轻便、不弯腰、能进角落、价格实在；
租房过渡型追求“轻便实用、快速搞定”；新中产美学型要求工具高颜值、能养护地面、散发香味，清洁即生活美学；宠物
主理型追求“防缠无死角、除菌净味”。
六大人群画像
租房过渡型
• 人物画像：21-30岁年轻房租客
• 清洁诉求：务实效率派，追求轻便、耐用、免手洗，
快速解决脏乱
• 清洁期待：一次拖净、小巧不占地
新中产美学
• 人物画像：中高收入、高学历、新一线美学生活家
• 清洁诉求：追求兼顾效率与美学的仪式，轻便、耐

用、售后、设计感、香氛缺一不可
• 清洁期待：无痕、免手洗、地面养护、香氛高颜值
宠物主理型
• 人物画像：养宠人群
• 清洁诉求：清洁视为守护家人的责任，最怕死角
藏毛、细菌、异味
• 清洁期待：无痕、防死角、不缠毛、免手洗可调节、
抑菌抗菌
效率至上型
• 人群画像：25-35岁都市白领
• 清洁诉求：健康与卫生意识超前，极度追求省时
省力，不愿在清洁上耗费精力
• 清洁期待：一步到位、防缠绕、无痕
银发务实型
• 人物画像：55岁以上
• 清洁诉求：极致简化，只要基本拖地功能，
轻便省力、能净死角、价格实惠
• 清洁期待：无痕、免手洗、不弯腰、不缠毛
品质苛求型
• 人物画像：36-45岁中高收入家庭
• 清洁诉求：视清洁为生活美学与治愈仪式，
追求完美无痕，愿为耐用工艺付费
• 清洁期待：无痕、防缠绕、深入缝隙清洁

11©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn

25-35岁都市白领健康与卫生意识显著超前、极度追求省力省时，不愿在清洁上耗费精力。这反映高线城市白领群体对居住
环境的高标准卫生要求；他们工作强度大、通勤时间长，可支配时间碎片化。他们希望用最短的时间、最少的体力完成清洁
任务，把精力留给工作、娱乐或休息。他们对于产品的需求务实理性，看重操作便捷性、齐全基础功能以及口碑。

都市白领效率至上，最无法容忍的是被打断 ——拖几下地就要去洗拖把、被毛发缠住需要手动清理、擦完还有异味水渍需要
返工。这些痛点直接拖慢效率，与他们对 "省时"的追求严重冲突。对于清洁产品的理想期待是清洁效果好、适应性强、毛发 /
头发吸附效果好，清洁工作一步到位、无需中途干预。

品质苛求型人群将清洁行为视为一种主动的生活美学表达，而非单纯的劳动。他们享受擦拭、整理、拖地过程中建立秩序、
掌控环境带来的心理满足，甚至将其作为工作之余的放松方式。他们愿意为清洁效果和工艺付费，认为好工具本身就是品质
生活的一部分。他们要求工具能快速且彻底地完成清洁，不留后患。

省力"；清洁习惯集中在早晨，以此作为开启一天的仪式感。

日常轻维护、周末深度清洁、突发状况清洁、婴宠家庭清洁有共性需求（高效、省力、无痕、自洁、除味、适应多场景），
也有差异化情感诉求。
清洁范围 局部、显眼区（地面、台面等） 全屋、死角、高处、各种针织物 点状、局部（餐桌、沙发、地毯等） 地面、家具、沙发、墙角、地毯

层污垢等 液体、油渍、呕吐物 /尿液等 毛发、尿液、涂鸦、呕吐物、碎屑等
时间压力 低，但希望快速完成 低，但希望一次彻底 极高，污渍停留越久越难清理 中等偏高，污渍可能被孩子或宠物
再次接触

日常维护的"轻清洁"：快节奏生活中的随手清洁
日常维护清洁中，简单扫地、拖地，是最普遍的基础清洁任务。消费者在使用现有清洁工具和方式时，普遍感受到杂物整理
耗精力、清洁效果不理想、工具本身难清洗等困扰。消费者期待理想清洁方案集中在清洁效果无痕无残留，即用即清、操作

便捷、收纳便利、体积小巧。

周末大扫除的"深清洁"：定期进行的系统性深度清洁
周末深度清洁不再局限于“地面拖扫”和“表面擦拭”，而是系统性、彻底性地处理家中长期积累的顽固脏污，如卫生间和
厨房的深度清洁。清洁的痛点比日常清洁更强烈、更集中，如清洁强度高、耗时耗力，顽固有无难以彻底请理等。消费者对
理想深度清洁方案的期待，已经超越了 "去污"本身，进入了对工具性能、劳动体验和健康安全的综合要求。
来源 ：N=500，于2026年5月通 过艾瑞智 研平台-消费 洞察数据 库调研 获得。

突发状况的"急救清洁"：意外污渍、紧急清洁的场景
突发状况的污渍停留越久，清理越难，消费者的心理焦虑也越强。突发状况的场景集中在日常饮食的撒漏、意外沾污污渍等。
突发状况清洁属于中低频家庭意外，发生频次以 "每月1次及以下"为主，也有相当比例的家庭每周1-2次。每次清洁时清洁时
长集中在5-15分钟，消费者希望面对突发状况清洁时能一擦就干净，无需反复擦拭；操作便捷，工具拿起来就用。

婴宠家庭清洁场景
婴宠家庭的"安心清洁"：突发、高难度、高卫生要求的清洁场景
婴宠家庭的日常清洁，除了常规的地面打扫外，也有特殊的突发清洁行为：孩子乱扔垃圾及乱涂乱画、宠物乱尿及呕吐物等
的处理。这些行为突发性强、污渍复杂且涉及到不同材质表面。婴宠家庭在清洁过程中，遇到的痛点比普通家庭更密集、更
棘手，如毛发缠绕严重、工具易脏且需中途多次清洗、清洁效果不佳等。婴宠家庭对理想清洁方案的期待，除了打扫干净外，
希望毛发吸附效果好，适应性强且清洗方便等。
来源 ：N=116，于2026年5月通 过艾瑞智 研平台-消费 洞察数据 库调研 获得。

消费者选购清洁用品时理性务实
多数消费者在选购清洁用品时表现得非常理性、务实，优先考虑 "能不能把清洁做好"、"是否省时省力"、"耐不耐用"、"值不
值这个价"。安全、除菌等进阶功能需求有一定关注，但并非主流决策因素。

未来展望
"享地"时代的清洁新图景05

### Page 42

42©2026.5iResearchInc.www.iresearch.com.cn
全球胶棉拖把正在回归材料科学
全球胶棉拖把的发展史是一部从结构改良、工具优化，最终回归材料科学的创新史。
宝家洁自主研发的欧科棉，如同一个分水岭：它证明了在胶棉拖把这个看似传统的红海市场，唯有从材料科学底层进行颠覆
式创新，才能彻底解决消费者痛点，开辟出全新的品类赛道。这正是 "中国制造"向"中国创造"跃迁的典型缩影。
来源 ：艾瑞消 费研究 院根据公 开资料整 理及绘 制
全球研发焦点从“制造出这种材料”
转向了“如何让这种材料更好”
传统PVA胶棉的“百年难题”
迎来终极破局者
起源与发明
（1920年-1960年）
性能探索与工艺优化
（1970年-2000年）
挑战、破局与新纪元
（2010年至今）
• 业界不断迭代发泡工艺，以解决成本高、耗水
量大且易造成污染的弊端。
• PVA胶棉的应用从家居清洁扩展到医疗、工业
和精密制造领域。
• 随着PVA胶棉材料的成熟，胶棉拖把开始在全
球普及，德国Vileda（微力达）、美国3M思

高（Scotch-Brite） 等国际品牌主导市场，
形成了欧美品牌主导的全球竞争格局。
• PVA “久放变硬，用前必泡”是百年行业难题。
同时在吸附毛发、清洁油污方面也存在明显短板。
• 长期以来，行业试图通过优化拖把结构来缓解
“变硬”带来的不便，但始终未能从材料本身攻
克这一物理定律。
• 宝家洁创新性地引入了柔性树脂，打破了传统胶
棉拖把“遇水则软，无水则硬”的固有模式，将
整个胶棉拖把行业带入了一个全新的纪元。
• 1924年：德国化学家合成出聚乙烯醇（PVA)。
• 二战后：日本研究人员成功攻克了PVA的耐水性问题。
• 1950年：日本企业实现了PVA纤维的工业化生产。
• 1960年代：日本企业将PVA开发成具有三维微孔结
构的PVF发泡海绵，其内部微孔结构能通过毛细现象
高效吸水，吸水率可达自身重量的8-10倍，正式拉
开了胶棉材料的商业应用序幕。
PVA材料完成了
从实验室发现到工业化生产的跨越
全球胶棉拖把发展史
